===== static/sw.js =====
SHA-256: 72e663a456fab2c5b104b098a6191196ef91ca69d4258ca08339748ad600568f

const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';
const CACHE_PREFIX = `esstu-pwa:${self.registration.scope}:`;
const SHELL_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}${CACHE_NAME}`;
const DATA_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}data-v2`;
const MAX_DATA_ENTRIES = 200;
const PIN_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}preferences-v1`;
const PIN_URL = new URL('offline-pins',self.registration.scope).href;
let protectedURLs = null;
async function loadPins(){if(!protectedURLs){const saved=await (await caches.open(PIN_CACHE)).match(PIN_URL);protectedURLs=new Set(saved?await saved.json():[]);}return protectedURLs;}
const SHELL = ['./', './index.html', './app.css', './shortcut-now.svg', './modern.js', './experience.js', './simple-ui.js', './study-tools.js', './accessibility.js', './manifest.json', './icons/app-icon.svg', './icons/app-icon-192.png', './icons/app-icon-512.png', './shortcut-now.png', './shortcut-rooms.png', './shortcut-session.png', './shortcut-campus.png'];
const SHELL_URLS = new Set(SHELL.map(path => new URL(path, self.registration.scope).href));
const API_URL = new URL('api/', self.registration.scope).href;
self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  event.waitUntil(caches.open(SHELL_CACHE).then(cache => cache.addAll(
    SHELL.map(path => new Request(new URL(path, self.registration.scope), {cache:'reload'}))
  )));
});
self.addEventListener('message', event => {
  if (event.data?.type === 'SKIP_WAITING') event.waitUntil(self.skipWaiting());
  if(event.data?.type==='PROTECT_DATA')event.waitUntil((async()=>{
    const urls=(event.data.urls || []).filter(url=>typeof url==='string' && url.startsWith(API_URL)).slice(0,64);
    protectedURLs=new Set(urls);await (await caches.open(PIN_CACHE)).put(PIN_URL,new Response(JSON.stringify(urls)));
  })());
});
async function trimData(cache) {
  const requests = await cache.keys();
  const pins=await loadPins();
  const victims=requests.filter(request=>!pins.has(request.url));
  // FIFO among unprotected responses; current/favourite endpoints are retained.
  await Promise.all(victims.slice(0,Math.max(0,requests.length-MAX_DATA_ENTRIES)).map(request=>cache.delete(request)));
}
// Serialize writes so simultaneous downloads cannot race the entry limit.
let dataWrite = Promise.resolve();
function saveData(cache, request, response) {
  dataWrite = dataWrite.catch(() => {}).then(async () => {
    const headers = new Headers(response.headers); headers.set('X-ESSTU-Schema', '2');
    await cache.put(request, new Response(response.body, {status:response.status,statusText:response.statusText,headers}));
    await trimData(cache);
  });
  return dataWrite;
}
async function migrateData(response) {
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') throw Error('Unsupported saved data');
  const markSchedule = schedule => {
    if (!schedule?.weeks) return;
    schedule.is_stale_fallback = true;
    schedule.source_mode = 'saved_snapshot';
    // Old data cannot establish that a teacher identity was checked.
    if (schedule.mode === 'teacher' && schedule.identity_ambiguous === undefined) schedule.identity_ambiguous = true;
  };
  markSchedule(payload);
  if (Array.isArray(payload.teachers)) payload.teachers.forEach(markSchedule);
  payload.schema_version = 2;
  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
  headers.set('Content-Type','application/json; charset=utf-8');
  headers.delete('Content-Length'); headers.delete('Content-Encoding'); headers.delete('ETag');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {status:response.status,headers});
}
self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    const data = await caches.open(DATA_CACHE);
    const legacyKeys = keys.filter(key => key.startsWith('esstu-live-') || key === `${CACHE_PREFIX}data-v1`);
    for (const key of legacyKeys.reverse()) {
      const legacy = await caches.open(key); let migrated = true, shared = false;
      for (const request of await legacy.keys()) {
        if (!request.url.startsWith(self.registration.scope)) { shared = true; continue; }
        if (!request.url.startsWith(API_URL) || await data.match(request)) continue;
        try { await saveData(data, request, await migrateData(await legacy.match(request))); }
        catch (error) { migrated = false; console.warn('ESSTU cache migration failed', error); }
      }
      if (migrated && !shared) await caches.delete(key);
    }
    await trimData(data);
    // Keep failed migrations and caches containing another application's entries.
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(key => key.startsWith(CACHE_PREFIX) && key !== SHELL_CACHE && key !== DATA_CACHE && key !== PIN_CACHE && !legacyKeys.includes(key)).map(key => caches.delete(key)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});
self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  const request = event.request, url = new URL(request.url);
  if (request.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== self.location.origin || !url.href.startsWith(self.registration.scope)) return;
  const isData = url.href.startsWith(API_URL);
  const assetURL = new URL(url); assetURL.search = ''; assetURL.hash = '';
  const isShell = SHELL_URLS.has(assetURL.href);
  // Other assets follow normal HTTP caching, never shell cache-first.
  if (!isData && !isShell) return;
  const background=[];
  const result=(async () => {
    const cache=await caches.open(isData?DATA_CACHE:SHELL_CACHE);
    const shellURL=request.mode==='navigate'?new URL('./',self.registration.scope).href:assetURL.href;
    if(!isData){const shell=await cache.match(shellURL);if(shell)return shell;}
    const saved=isData?await cache.match(request):null;
    if(saved && request.headers.get('X-ESSTU-Cache-Only')==='1')return saved;
    const fetching=fetch(request,{cache:'reload',signal:saved?undefined:AbortSignal.timeout(12000)}).then(async response=>{
      if(response.ok){
        try{
          const changed=isData && saved && await saved.clone().text()!==await response.clone().text();
          if(isData)await saveData(cache,request,response.clone());else await cache.put(shellURL,response.clone());
          if(changed)for(const client of await self.clients.matchAll({type:'window'}))client.postMessage({type:'DATA_UPDATED',url:request.url});
        }catch(error){console.warn('ESSTU cache write failed',error);}
      }
      return response;
    });
    // Keep a slow transfer alive after serving a saved response.
    background.push(fetching.catch(()=>{}));
    try{
      let response;
      if(saved){
        let timer;
        try{response=await Promise.race([fetching,new Promise(resolve=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve(null),3500);})]);}
        finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
        if(!response)return cachedResponse(saved,'refreshing');
      }else response=await fetching;
      return saved && response.status>=500?cachedResponse(saved,'server-error'):response;
    }catch{return saved?cachedResponse(saved,'offline'):new Response('Нет сохранённой копии. Подключитесь к интернету.',{status:503});}
  })();
  event.respondWith(result);
  event.waitUntil(result.then(()=>Promise.all(background)).catch(()=>{}));
});

function cachedResponse(saved, reason) {
  const headers = new Headers(saved.headers); headers.set('X-ESSTU-Cache', reason);
  return new Response(saved.clone().body, {status:saved.status,statusText:saved.statusText,headers});
}


===== scripts/build_static_site.py =====
SHA-256: b832273cdb73199845617af32c3a6d16202b2c57d08d994260d089628a0272ab

import hashlib
from collections import Counter
import json
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import time
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.catalog import build_catalog, source_metadata, stamp_schedule
from esstu_schedule.data.repository import (
    group_schedule as parse_group_schedule_html,
    group_identity_error,
)
from esstu_schedule.parser import (
    SECTIONS,
    get_week_num,
    parse_groups_list_html,
    normalize_group_name,
)
from esstu_schedule.rooms import build_rooms_index
from esstu_schedule.teachers import departments_from_teachers, teachers_from_groups

from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import (
    parsing_status,
    record_error,
    reset_diagnostics,
)
from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.data.provenance import build_provenance

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEMO_DIR = BASE_DIR / "demo_data"
STATIC_DIR = BASE_DIR / "static"
DIST_DIR = BASE_DIR / "dist"


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def shell_paths(dist):
    return [
        "./",
        *[
            "./" + path.relative_to(dist).as_posix()
            for path in sorted(dist.rglob("*"))
            if path.is_file()
            and path.relative_to(dist).parts[0] != "api"
            and path != dist / "sw.js"
        ],
    ]


def build_id(dist: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for path in sorted(dist.rglob("*")):
        relative = path.relative_to(dist)
        if not path.is_file() or relative.parts[0] == "api":
            continue
        content = path.read_bytes()
        if relative == Path("index.html"):
            content = re.sub(
                rb'content="[^"]+" name="app-version"',
                b'content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version"',
                content,
            )
        if relative == Path("sw.js"):
            content = re.sub(
                rb"const CACHE_NAME = '[^']+';",
                b"const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';",
                content,
            )
            content = re.sub(
                rb"const SHELL = \[.*?\];",
                ("const SHELL = " + json.dumps(shell_paths(dist)) + ";").encode(),
                content,
            )
        digest.update(relative.as_posix().encode())
        digest.update(b"\0")
        digest.update(len(content).to_bytes(8, "big"))
        digest.update(content)
    return digest.hexdigest()[:16]


def stamp_shell(dist: Path) -> str:
    identity = build_id(dist)
    worker = dist / "sw.js"
    worker_text, worker_count = re.subn(
        r"const CACHE_NAME = '[^']+';",
        f"const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-{identity}';",
        worker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
    )
    index = dist / "index.html"
    index_text, index_count = re.subn(
        r'content="[^"]+" name="app-version"',
        f'content="{identity}" name="app-version"',
        index.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
    )
    if worker_count != 1 or index_count != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Expected exactly one CACHE_NAME and app-version marker; shell was not stamped"
        )
    worker_text = re.sub(
        r"const SHELL = \[.*?\];",
        "const SHELL = " + json.dumps(shell_paths(dist)) + ";",
        worker_text,
    )
    worker.write_text(worker_text, encoding="utf-8")
    index.write_text(index_text, encoding="utf-8")
    return identity


def configure_console():
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
            stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="backslashreplace")


def source_modified_iso(value):
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        parsed = parsedate_to_datetime(value)
        return parsed.astimezone(UTC).isoformat() if parsed.tzinfo else None
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        try:
            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            return parsed.astimezone(UTC).isoformat() if parsed.tzinfo else None
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return None


def write_api_json(path, payload):
    write_json_atomic(path, {**payload, "schema_version": 2})


def build():
    configure_console()
    reset_diagnostics()
    css_path = STATIC_DIR / "app.css"
    if not css_path.is_file() or not (
        css_path.stat().st_size >= 8000
        and "tailwindcss" in css_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        and "--campus-bg" in css_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Сначала соберите полный CSS: npm run build:css")
    print("🚀 Starting static build for GitHub Pages...")
    start_time = time.time()

    if DIST_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(DIST_DIR)
    DIST_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Copy static assets (index.html, manifest.json, sw.js, icons)
    for item in STATIC_DIR.iterdir():
        if item.is_file():
            shutil.copy2(item, DIST_DIR / item.name)
        elif item.is_dir():
            shutil.copytree(item, DIST_DIR / item.name)
    print("✓ Copied static files to dist/")

    # 2. Create API directory structure
    api_dir = DIST_DIR / "api"
    api_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "groups").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "schedule").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "departments").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "teachers" / "all").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "department").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Status
    built_at = datetime.now(UTC)
    generated_at = built_at.isoformat()
    now_week = get_week_num(built_at)
    status_payload = {
        "university": "ВСГУТУ",
        "timezone": "Asia/Irkutsk (UTC+8)",
        "server_time_ulan_ude": built_at.astimezone(
            timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
        ).isoformat(),
        "week_calculated_at": generated_at,
        **now_week,
    }

    # 3. Parse and save groups list for all sections
    from scripts.publication import previous_groups, last_good_group

    previous = previous_groups(os.environ.get("ESSTU_BASELINE_DIR"))
    group_schedules = []
    group_expected = 0
    group_failures = []
    metadata = source_metadata(DEMO_DIR, with_hashes=True)
    failed_downloads = {
        row["path"]
        for report in DEMO_DIR.glob("*sync.json")
        for row in json.loads(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("failed", [])
        if "path" in row
    }
    for section in SECTIONS.keys():
        rasp_file = DEMO_DIR / section / "raspisan.htm"
        if not rasp_file.exists():
            record_error(f"{section}/raspisan.htm", "Отсутствует каталог групп")
            continue
        html = rasp_file.read_bytes().decode("cp1251", errors="replace")
        groups = parse_groups_list_html(html, section=section)
        group_expected += len(groups)
        payload = {"section": section, "count": len(groups), "groups": groups}
        write_api_json(api_dir / "groups" / f"{section}.json", payload)
        print(f"✓ Saved {len(groups)} groups for section '{section}'")

        # 4. Parse group schedules
        sec_sched_dir = api_dir / "schedule" / section
        sec_sched_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        count_sched = 0
        for group in groups:
            gid = group["id"]
            htm_file = DEMO_DIR / section / f"{gid}.htm"
            if not htm_file.exists():
                record_error(f"{section}/{gid}.htm", "Отсутствует расписание группы")
                group_failures.append(
                    {"source": f"{section}/{gid}.htm", "reason": "missing_file"}
                )
                missing = {
                    "mode": "student",
                    "group_id": gid,
                    "group_name": group["name"],
                    "section": section,
                    "is_incomplete": True,
                    "parse_errors": ["Отсутствует расписание группы"],
                    "weeks": {"1": {}, "2": {}},
                    "total_lessons": 0,
                }
                sched = last_good_group(
                    missing,
                    previous.get((section, normalize_group_name(group["name"]))),
                    built_at,
                )
                group_schedules.append(sched)
                write_api_json(sec_sched_dir / f"{gid}.json", sched)
            if htm_file.exists():
                try:
                    g_html = htm_file.read_bytes().decode("cp1251", errors="replace")
                    sched = parse_group_schedule_html(
                        g_html, group_id=gid, section=section
                    )
                    identity_error = group_identity_error(sched, group["name"])
                    if identity_error:
                        group_failures.append(
                            {
                                "source": f"{section}/{gid}.htm",
                                "reason": identity_error,
                                "expected_name": group["name"],
                                "parsed_name": sched["group_name"],
                                "bytes": htm_file.stat().st_size,
                                "sha256": metadata.get(f"{section}/{gid}.htm", {}).get(
                                    "sha256"
                                ),
                            }
                        )
                        record_error(
                            f"{section}/{gid}.htm",
                            "Имя группы не совпадает с каталогом",
                        )
                        sched.update(
                            group_name=group["name"],
                            norm_group_name=group["norm_name"],
                            weeks={"1": {}, "2": {}},
                            total_lessons=0,
                            is_incomplete=True,
                            parse_errors=["Имя группы не совпадает с каталогом"],
                        )
                    if sched.get("is_incomplete") and not identity_error:
                        group_failures.append(
                            {
                                "source": f"{section}/{gid}.htm",
                                "reason": "table_structure",
                                "errors": sched.get("parse_errors"),
                                "bytes": htm_file.stat().st_size,
                            }
                        )
                    sched["resolved_group_id"] = gid
                    sched["last_modified"] = source_modified_iso(
                        metadata.get(f"{section}/{gid}.htm", {}).get("last_modified")
                    )
                    sched["week_now"] = now_week
                    sched["week_calculated_at"] = generated_at
                    stamp_schedule(sched, metadata, generated_at)
                    if (
                        os.environ.get("ESSTU_DATA_UPDATE") == "1"
                        and f"{section}/{gid}.htm" in failed_downloads
                    ):
                        sched.update(
                            is_stale_fallback=True,
                            fallback_reason=["Не удалось обновить источник"],
                        )
                    if os.environ.get("ESSTU_DATA_UPDATE") == "1" and not sched.get(
                        "is_incomplete"
                    ):
                        from scripts.publication import source_is_recent

                        if not source_is_recent(sched, built_at):
                            sched.update(
                                is_incomplete=True,
                                parse_errors=[
                                    "Источник старше 7 дней или дата проверки неизвестна"
                                ],
                                weeks={"1": {}, "2": {}},
                                total_lessons=0,
                            )
                            group_failures.append(
                                {
                                    "source": f"{section}/{gid}.htm",
                                    "reason": "source_expired",
                                }
                            )
                    sched = last_good_group(
                        sched,
                        previous.get((section, normalize_group_name(group["name"]))),
                        built_at,
                    )
                    group_schedules.append(sched)
                    write_api_json(sec_sched_dir / f"{gid}.json", sched)
                    count_sched += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    record_error(f"{section}/{gid}.htm", str(e))
                    LOGGER.exception("Group parsing failed: %s/%s", section, gid)
                    group_failures.append(
                        {
                            "source": f"{section}/{gid}.htm",
                            "reason": "parse_exception",
                            "error": str(e),
                        }
                    )
        print(f"✓ Generated {count_sched} schedule JSON files for '{section}'")

    catalog, institutes, departments = build_catalog(
        DEMO_DIR, teachers_from_groups(group_schedules)
    )
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet"):
        write_api_json(
            api_dir / "teachers" / "all" / f"{section}.json",
            {
                "count": len(catalog),
                "teachers": catalog,
                "generated_at": generated_at,
            },
        )
        write_api_json(
            api_dir / "departments" / f"{section}.json",
            {
                "institutes": institutes[section] or departments_from_teachers(catalog),
                "generated_at": generated_at,
            },
        )
    for section, caf, payload in departments:
        target = api_dir / "department" / section
        target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        write_api_json(target / f"{caf}.json", payload)
    print(
        f"Official department files: {len(departments)}; combined teachers: {len(catalog)}"
    )

    room_sources = [*group_schedules, *catalog]
    sync_status = (
        json.loads((DEMO_DIR / "department-sync.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if (DEMO_DIR / "department-sync.json").exists()
        else {}
    )
    provenance = build_provenance(metadata, BASE_DIR)
    write_json_atomic(api_dir / "sources.json", metadata)
    diagnostics = {**parsing_status(), **provenance}
    diagnostics["incomplete_schedule_count"] = sum(
        bool(item.get("is_incomplete")) for item in [*group_schedules, *catalog]
    )
    diagnostics["group_failures"] = group_failures
    diagnostics["group_failure_reasons"] = dict(
        Counter(row["reason"] for row in group_failures)
    )
    diagnostics["fallback_group_count"] = sum(
        item.get("source_mode") == "last_good_json" for item in group_schedules
    )
    diagnostics["group_expected_count"] = group_expected
    diagnostics["group_failed_count"] = group_expected - sum(
        not item.get("is_incomplete") for item in group_schedules
    )
    write_api_json(
        api_dir / "catalog-status.json",
        {**sync_status, **diagnostics, "generated_at": generated_at},
    )
    identity = stamp_shell(DIST_DIR)
    write_api_json(
        api_dir / "status.json",
        {
            **status_payload,
            **diagnostics,
            "generated_at": generated_at,
            "build_id": identity,
        },
    )
    rooms_payload = {
        **build_rooms_index(room_sources),
        "generated_at": generated_at,
        "parse_error_count": diagnostics["parse_error_count"],
        "schema_version": 2,
    }
    write_api_json(api_dir / "rooms.json", rooms_payload)
    # Runtime index survives replacing dist/ during the next background build.
    runtime_root = Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_SNAPSHOT_DIR", BASE_DIR / "cache_store"))
    write_json_atomic(
        Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_ROOMS_INDEX", runtime_root / "rooms.json")),
        rooms_payload,
    )
    print(
        f"🎉 Build complete in {time.time() - start_time:.2f}s! All output saved to dist/"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    build()


===== scripts/check_sync.py =====
SHA-256: 30667d7373e7edbe4783cdbcc542edec178d4caee93673066a0eedbf9631cb8f

"""A partial failed download is visible to Actions and cannot be published as success."""

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path


def check_reports(root, allow_partial=False):
    validated = 0
    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        report = json.loads((root / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        validated += len(report.get("downloaded", []))
        if not allow_partial and (not report.get("downloaded") or report.get("failed")):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"{name}: downloaded={len(report.get('downloaded', []))}; failed={len(report.get('failed', []))}"
            )

    if not validated:
        raise RuntimeError("No source validated; keep the previous data publication")


def check_parse_quality(site, max_error_rate=None, baseline=None, max_regression=None):
    if max_error_rate is None:
        max_error_rate = float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_ERROR_RATE", "0.25"))
    if max_regression is None:
        max_regression = float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_REGRESSION", "0.01"))
    if not 0 <= max_regression <= 1:
        raise ValueError("max_regression must be between 0 and 1")
    if not 0 <= max_error_rate <= 1:
        raise ValueError("max_error_rate must be between 0 and 1")
    status = json.loads((Path(site) / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    total, failed = (
        status.get("group_expected_count", 0),
        status.get("group_failed_count", 0),
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(total, int)
        or total <= 0
        or not isinstance(failed, int)
        or not 0 <= failed <= total
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Parser coverage is missing or invalid")
    ratio = failed / total
    allowed = max_error_rate
    if baseline and (Path(baseline) / "api/status.json").exists():
        previous = json.loads(
            (Path(baseline) / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        previous_total = previous.get("group_expected_count", 0)
        previous_failed = previous.get("group_failed_count", 0)
        catalogs = list((Path(baseline) / "api/groups").glob("*.json"))
        if catalogs:
            from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_identity_error

            previous_total = previous_failed = 0
            for catalog in catalogs:
                for group in json.loads(catalog.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["groups"]:
                    previous_total += 1
                    path = (
                        Path(baseline)
                        / "api/schedule"
                        / catalog.stem
                        / (str(group["id"]) + ".json")
                    )
                    old = (
                        json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                        if path.exists()
                        else {"is_incomplete": True}
                    )
                    previous_failed += bool(
                        old.get("is_incomplete")
                        or group_identity_error(old, group["name"])
                    )
        if previous_total:
            allowed = min(allowed, previous_failed / previous_total + max_regression)
    if ratio > allowed:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Publication blocked: {failed}/{total} group schedules failed ({ratio:.1%}), allowed {allowed:.1%}"
        )
    print(f"Parser quality OK: {failed}/{total} group schedules failed ({ratio:.1%})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--allow-partial", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-error-rate",
        type=float,
        default=float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_ERROR_RATE", "0.25")),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.site:
        check_parse_quality(args.site, args.max_error_rate, args.baseline)
    else:
        check_reports(
            Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "demo_data", args.allow_partial
        )


===== scripts/publication.py =====
SHA-256: 4e722c2daa66401710a90573ef38ea623ac2ccc7ef3ff7c8761303a3077a280a

"""Select per-group fallbacks and keep code-only releases independent of data quality."""

import argparse
import json
import shutil
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.parser import normalize_group_name


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def source_is_recent(schedule, now, max_age_days=7):
    try:
        checked = datetime.fromisoformat(
            (schedule.get("source_checked_at") or "").replace("Z", "+00:00")
        )
        return timedelta(0) <= now - checked <= timedelta(days=max_age_days)
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


def last_good_group(candidate, previous, now=None, max_age_days=7):
    if not candidate.get("is_incomplete") or not previous:
        return candidate
    if not any(character.isalnum() for character in candidate.get("group_name", "")):
        return candidate
    if previous.get("is_incomplete") or normalize_group_name(
        previous.get("group_name", "")
    ) != normalize_group_name(candidate.get("group_name", "")):
        return candidate
    try:
        checked = datetime.fromisoformat(
            (previous.get("source_checked_at") or "").replace("Z", "+00:00")
        )
        age = (now or datetime.now(UTC)) - checked
        if not timedelta(0) <= age <= timedelta(days=max_age_days):
            return candidate
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return candidate
    result = deepcopy(previous)
    result.update(
        group_id=candidate["group_id"],
        resolved_group_id=candidate["group_id"],
        is_stale_fallback=True,
        source_mode="last_good_json",
        fallback_reason=candidate.get("parse_errors", []),
        origin_source_file=previous.get("source_file"),
    )
    return result


def previous_groups(root):
    if not root:
        return {}
    groups = {}
    for path in (Path(root) / "api/schedule").glob("*/*.json"):
        item = read_json(path)
        if not item.get("is_incomplete"):
            groups[
                (item.get("section"), normalize_group_name(item.get("group_name", "")))
            ] = item
    return groups


def reuse_api(site, baseline, expected_build):
    site, baseline = Path(site), Path(baseline)
    current = read_json(site / "api/status.json")
    if current["build_id"] != expected_build:
        raise RuntimeError("Publication shell differs from tested shell")
    previous = read_json(baseline / "api/status.json")
    # Stage the complete previous API before replacing anything in the new site.
    staged = site / "previous-api"
    shutil.copytree(baseline / "api", staged)
    shutil.rmtree(site / "api")
    staged.rename(site / "api")
    previous.update(
        build_id=expected_build,
        code_revision=current["code_revision"],
        code_deployed_at=current["generated_at"],
    )
    write_json_atomic(site / "api/status.json", previous)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path, default=Path("dist"))
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["code", "data"], required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-build", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    status = read_json(args.site / "api/status.json")
    if status["build_id"] != args.expected_build:
        raise RuntimeError("Publication shell differs from tested shell")
    if (
        args.mode == "code"
        and args.baseline
        and (args.baseline / "api/status.json").exists()
    ):
        reuse_api(args.site, args.baseline, args.expected_build)
        print(
            "Code release: previous API reused byte-for-byte except shell provenance in status"
        )
    elif (
        args.mode == "code"
        and __import__("os").environ.get("GITHUB_REF") == "refs/heads/main"
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Code publication requires previous published API or durable data branch"
        )
    elif args.mode == "code":
        print(
            "Review build: committed test sources retained; data quality gate applies only to data releases"
        )
    else:
        from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality

        check_parse_quality(args.site, baseline=args.baseline)


===== scripts/data_branch.py =====
SHA-256: f51613136d51610aefcf0ba987b8a9495c1b020b736f86064d28058746a4dc69

"""Durable validated data snapshots, stored separately from application history."""

import argparse
import base64
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


def git(root, *arguments, check=True):
    environment = os.environ.copy()
    if environment.get("GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        environment.update(
            GIT_CONFIG_COUNT="1",
            GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0="http.https://github.com/.extraheader",
            GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0="AUTHORIZATION: basic "
            + base64.b64encode(
                ("x-access-token:" + environment["GITHUB_TOKEN"]).encode()
            ).decode(),
        )
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", *arguments], cwd=root, env=environment, capture_output=True, text=True
    )
    if check and result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("Data branch git operation failed: " + arguments[0])
    return result


def restore(directory, repository, sources):
    directory = Path(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    git(directory, "init", "-q")
    git(directory, "config", "core.autocrlf", "false")
    git(directory, "remote", "add", "origin", repository)
    available = git(
        directory, "ls-remote", "--heads", "origin", "refs/heads/data"
    ).stdout.strip()
    if not available:
        from scripts.published_data import bootstrap

        owner, repo = repository.removesuffix(".git").rstrip("/").split("/")[-2:]
        bootstrap(directory, f"https://{owner}.github.io/{repo}/")
        return False
    git(directory, "fetch", "--depth=1", "origin", "data")
    git(directory, "checkout", "-B", "data", "FETCH_HEAD")
    if not (directory / "api/status.json").exists():
        raise RuntimeError("Data branch is missing a validated API")
    shutil.copytree(directory / "sources", sources, dirs_exist_ok=True)
    print("Restored durable validated sources and API")
    return True


def save(directory, site, sources, revision):
    directory = Path(directory)
    for name, source in [("api", Path(site) / "api"), ("sources", Path(sources))]:
        target = directory / name
        if target.resolve() == source.resolve():
            continue
        if target.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(target)
        shutil.copytree(source, target)
    (directory / "README.md").write_text(
        "Validated API and source snapshots. Application revision: " + revision + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    git(directory, "config", "user.name", "github-actions[bot]")
    git(
        directory,
        "config",
        "user.email",
        "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com",
    )
    git(directory, "add", "api", "sources", "README.md")
    if git(directory, "diff", "--cached", "--quiet", check=False).returncode == 0:
        print("Data unchanged")
        return
    git(directory, "checkout", "-B", "data")
    git(
        directory, "commit", "-m", "Update validated schedule data for " + revision[:12]
    )
    git(directory, "push", "origin", "HEAD:refs/heads/data")
    print("Validated data saved in branch data")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("mode", choices=["restore", "save", "checkpoint"])
    parser.add_argument("--directory", type=Path, default=Path("preview/data"))
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path, default=Path("dist"))
    parser.add_argument("--sources", type=Path, default=Path("demo_data"))
    parser.add_argument(
        "--repository",
        default="https://github.com/"
        + os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "stasdondukov2-sys/esstu-schedule")
        + ".git",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.mode == "restore":
        restore(args.directory, args.repository, args.sources)
    elif args.mode == "checkpoint":
        save(
            args.directory,
            args.directory,
            args.sources,
            os.environ.get("GITHUB_SHA", "local"),
        )
    else:
        save(
            args.directory,
            args.site,
            args.sources,
            os.environ.get("GITHUB_SHA", "local"),
        )


===== scripts/published_data.py =====
SHA-256: 4665ebac113785cfc2a48e4b992f7859fd7e782663e1a4f0acc4b2c3c5948842

"""Bootstrap an immutable API snapshot from the currently published Pages site."""

import json
import urllib.request
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic


def bootstrap(directory, base):
    api = Path(directory) / "api"

    def fetch(path):
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            base.rstrip("/") + "/" + path,
            headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache", "User-Agent": "ESSTU-Schedule/2.5"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read())

    status = fetch("api/status.json")
    paths = [
        "api/groups/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2")
    ]
    paths += [
        "api/teachers/all/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
    ]
    paths += [
        "api/departments/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
    ]
    paths += ["api/rooms.json", "api/sources.json", "api/catalog-status.json"]
    payloads = {}

    def download(path):
        value = fetch(path)
        write_json_atomic(Path(directory) / path, value)
        return path, value

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
        for path, value in pool.map(download, paths):
            payloads[path] = value
        schedules = [
            f"api/schedule/{section}/{group['id']}.json"
            for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2")
            for group in payloads[f"api/groups/{section}.json"]["groups"]
        ]
        departments = [
            f"api/department/{section}/{department['caf_id']}.json"
            for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
            for institute in payloads[f"api/departments/{section}.json"]["institutes"]
            for department in institute["departments"]
        ]
        list(pool.map(download, sorted(set(schedules + departments))))
    after = fetch("api/status.json")
    if any(
        after.get(key) != status.get(key)
        for key in ("generated_at", "sources_sha256", "code_revision")
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Published data changed during bootstrap; retry using one complete release"
        )
    status.setdefault("group_expected_count", len(schedules))
    status.setdefault(
        "group_failed_count",
        sum(
            json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("is_incomplete", False)
            for path in (api / "schedule").glob("*/*.json")
        ),
    )
    status["baseline_origin"] = base
    write_json_atomic(api / "status.json", status)
    print("Bootstrapped published API:", len(schedules), "groups")


===== scripts/sync_departments.py =====
SHA-256: 9e43c111657115409484e0d834a7de9409078ef0edfec77bbd170f37c2f53c5c

"""Download only official department pages; retain the last good copy on failure."""

import json
import hashlib
import logging
import re
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import os
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.repository import (
    department_schedules as parse_department_teachers_html,
)
from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_schedule as parse_group_schedule_html
from esstu_schedule.parser import parse_departments_list_html, parse_groups_list_html
from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_identity_error
from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "demo_data"
SECTIONS = ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")


def validate_page(relative, raw):
    html = raw.decode("cp1251", errors="replace")
    section, name = relative.split("/")
    if name == "craspisanEdt.htm":
        valid = parse_departments_list_html(html, section)
    elif name == "raspisan.htm":
        valid = parse_groups_list_html(html, section)
    elif re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.htm", name):
        schedule = parse_group_schedule_html(html, name[:-4], section)
        catalog = ROOT / section / "raspisan.htm"
        groups = (
            parse_groups_list_html(catalog.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section)
            if catalog.exists()
            else []
        )
        expected = next(
            (group["name"] for group in groups if group["id"] == name[:-4]), None
        )
        if expected is None or group_identity_error(schedule, expected):
            raise ValueError("Group identity does not match the current catalog")
        valid = not schedule.get("is_incomplete") and bool(
            schedule["weeks"]["1"] or schedule["weeks"]["2"]
        )
    else:
        valid = parse_department_teachers_html(html, name[:-4], section)
    if isinstance(valid, list) and any(item.get("is_incomplete") for item in valid):
        valid = False
    if not valid:
        raise ValueError("Page contains no valid schedule data")


def fetch_page(relative):
    target = ROOT / relative
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    old = manifest.get(relative, {})
    previous = target.read_bytes() if target.exists() else None
    valid_saved = previous is not None and hashlib.sha256(
        previous
    ).hexdigest() == old.get("sha256")
    failed_before = any(
        relative == row.get("path")
        for report in ROOT.glob("*sync.json")
        for row in json.loads(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("failed", [])
    )
    if (
        valid_saved
        and not failed_before
        and relative.split("/")[-1] not in ("raspisan.htm", "craspisanEdt.htm")
    ):
        try:
            age = (
                datetime.now(UTC) - datetime.fromisoformat(old["retrieved_at"])
            ).total_seconds()
            if 0 <= age < 25 * 60:
                validate_page(relative, previous)
                return relative, {**old, "reused": True}
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            pass
    headers = {"User-Agent": "ESSTU-Schedule/2.5"}
    if valid_saved:
        if old.get("etag"):
            headers["If-None-Match"] = old["etag"]
        if old.get("last_modified"):
            headers["If-Modified-Since"] = old["last_modified"]
    for attempt in range(3):
        try:
            request = urllib.request.Request(
                "https://portal.esstu.ru/" + relative, headers=headers
            )
            try:
                with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
                    raw = response.read()
                    modified = response.headers.get("Last-Modified")
                    etag = response.headers.get("ETag")
                    length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
                    if length and int(length) != len(raw):
                        raise OSError("Truncated HTTP response")
            except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
                if error.code != 304 or not valid_saved:
                    raise
                raw = previous
                modified = old.get("last_modified")
                etag = old.get("etag")
            validate_page(relative, raw)
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if raw != previous:
                temporary = target.with_suffix(target.suffix + ".tmp")
                temporary.write_bytes(raw)
                os.replace(temporary, target)
            return relative, {
                "retrieved_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
                "last_modified": modified,
                "etag": etag,
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                "bytes": len(raw),
                "reused": raw == previous,
            }
        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as error:
            if isinstance(error, urllib.error.HTTPError) and error.code < 500:
                raise
            if attempt == 2:
                raise
            time.sleep(0.5 * 2**attempt)


def sync():
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    targets, failures, successes = [], [], []
    for section in SECTIONS:
        relative = f"{section}/craspisanEdt.htm"
        try:
            path, meta = fetch_page(relative)
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                "Source synchronization failed: %s", relative
            )
            failures.append({"path": relative, "error": str(exc)})
            # A failed live catalog is not a reason to scan every stale page on an unreachable host.
            continue
        index = ROOT / relative
        if index.exists():
            for institute in parse_departments_list_html(
                index.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section
            ):
                for department in institute["departments"]:
                    if re.fullmatch(r"Caf\d+", department["caf_id"], re.IGNORECASE):
                        targets.append(f"{section}/{department['caf_id']}.htm")
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
        pending = {pool.submit(fetch_page, path): path for path in sorted(set(targets))}
        for future in as_completed(pending):
            try:
                path, meta = future.result()
                manifest[path] = meta
                write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
                successes.append(path)
            except Exception as exc:
                logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                    "Source synchronization failed: %s", pending[future]
                )
                failures.append({"path": pending[future], "error": str(exc)})
    write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
    report = {
        "checked_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "downloaded": successes,
        "failed": failures,
        "department_pages_expected": len(set(targets)),
    }
    write_json_atomic(ROOT / "department-sync.json", report)
    print(f"Official pages downloaded: {len(successes)}; failed: {len(failures)}")
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sync()


===== scripts/sync_esstu.py =====
SHA-256: f1b87c431e3623dc335fa6171bedbcd928fdf499ab51ce95cfde183500b3fc49

"""Refresh official department and group snapshots without generating test data."""

import json
import logging
import re
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.parser import SECTIONS, parse_groups_list_html
from scripts.sync_departments import ROOT, fetch_page
from scripts.sync_departments import sync as sync_departments


def sync_groups():
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    targets, failures, successes = [], [], []
    for section in SECTIONS:
        relative = f"{section}/raspisan.htm"
        try:
            path, meta = fetch_page(relative)
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                "Source synchronization failed: %s", relative
            )
            failures.append({"path": relative, "error": str(exc)})
            continue
        index = ROOT / relative
        if index.exists():
            groups = parse_groups_list_html(
                index.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section
            )
            targets.extend(
                f"{section}/{g['id']}.htm"
                for g in groups
                if re.fullmatch(r"\d+", str(g["id"]))
            )
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
        pending = {pool.submit(fetch_page, path): path for path in sorted(set(targets))}
        for future in as_completed(pending):
            try:
                path, meta = future.result()
                manifest[path] = meta
                write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
                successes.append(path)
                if len(successes) % 50 == 0:
                    print(
                        f"Group synchronization progress: {len(successes)} pages downloaded",
                        flush=True,
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                    "Source synchronization failed: %s", pending[future]
                )
                failures.append({"path": pending[future], "error": str(exc)})
    write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
    report = {
        "checked_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "downloaded": successes,
        "failed": failures,
        "group_pages_expected": len(set(targets)),
    }
    write_json_atomic(ROOT / "group-sync.json", report)
    print(f"Group pages downloaded: {len(successes)}; failed: {len(failures)}")
    return report


def sync_all():
    sync_departments()
    return sync_groups()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sync_all()


===== scripts/freshness.cjs =====
SHA-256: 8f03a17b2528709b2f4bb8d09cdd64489fe6e3eb0ec71e7af45da6c65cc68715

const TITLE='Расписание: выгрузка устарела';
function freshnessProblem(status,now=Date.now()){
  const issues=[];
  for(const [key,label] of [['generated_at','Сборка'],['source_checked_at_latest','Проверка источников']]){
    const stamp=Date.parse(status?.[key]);
    if(!Number.isFinite(stamp))issues.push(`${label}: дата неизвестна`);
    else if(stamp>now+300000)issues.push(`${label}: дата в будущем`);
    else if(now-stamp>12*3600000)issues.push(`${label}: более 12 часов назад (${new Date(stamp).toISOString()})`);
  }
  return issues.join('\n');
}
async function monitor({github,context,fetchImpl=fetch,now=Date.now()}){
  const {owner,repo}=context.repo;
  const url=`https://${owner}.github.io/${repo}/api/status.json`;
  let problem;
  try{
    const response=await fetchImpl(`${url}?freshness=${now}`,{cache:'no-store',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
    if(!response.ok)throw Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
    problem=freshnessProblem(await response.json(),now);
  }catch(error){problem=`Не удалось проверить опубликованный status.json: ${error.message}`;}
  const issues=await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo,{owner,repo,state:'open',creator:'github-actions[bot]'});
  const existing=issues.find(issue=>!issue.pull_request && issue.title===TITLE);
  if(problem){
    const body=`Опубликованные данные требуют проверки.\n\n${problem}\n\n[Статус сайта](${url}) · [Проверка](https://github.com/${owner}/${repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}).`;
    if(existing)await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,body});
    else await github.rest.issues.create({owner,repo,title:TITLE,body});
  }else if(existing)await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,state:'closed',body:`Свежесть опубликованной выгрузки восстановлена. [Статус сайта](${url}).`});
  return problem;
}
module.exports={freshnessProblem,monitor};


===== scripts/smoke_site.py =====
SHA-256: 9ee1ffef6625a394409de7c6d680f889c35429c51fbde4ce05a9fb82eb3f25a7

"""Validate a publication without tying live portal data to browser fixtures."""

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path


def check_site(root: Path, now: datetime | None = None, minimum_groups: int = 100):
    now = now or datetime.now(UTC)
    status = json.loads((root / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    generated = datetime.fromisoformat(
        status.get("code_deployed_at", status["generated_at"]).replace("Z", "+00:00")
    )
    if generated.tzinfo is None or not -300 <= (now - generated).total_seconds() <= 900:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Publication status is missing a recent timezone-aware generated_at"
        )
    total = 0
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        groups = json.loads(
            (root / f"api/groups/{section}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )["groups"]
        if not groups:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Empty group catalog: {section}")
        total += len(groups)
        for group in groups:
            path = root / f"api/schedule/{section}/{group['id']}.json"
            if not path.is_file():
                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing schedule: {section}/{group['id']}")
    if total < minimum_groups:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Too few groups: {total} < {minimum_groups}")
    if status.get("schema_version", 1) not in (1, 2) or not status.get("build_id"):
        raise RuntimeError("Missing publication schema/build identity")
    print(
        f"Publication smoke OK: {total} groups, recent status, compatible schema, build {status['build_id']}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    check_site(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "dist")


===== scripts/verify_deployment.py =====
SHA-256: e6dcdc7b6c7566794e29dba9b05d57f37c3f6e52d53f4dc76b2c910b7152b26e

"""Assert that deployed HTML and API belong to the tested shell."""

import argparse
import json
import re
import time
import urllib.request


def verify(base, expected, attempts=5):
    for attempt in range(attempts):
        try:

            def fetch(path):
                request = urllib.request.Request(
                    base.rstrip("/")
                    + "/"
                    + path
                    + "?verification="
                    + str(time.time_ns()),
                    headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"},
                )
                with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
                    return response.read().decode("utf-8")

            status = json.loads(fetch("api/status.json"))
            html = fetch("index.html")
            version = re.search(r'content="([^"]+)" name="app-version"', html)
            if (
                status.get("build_id") != expected
                or not version
                or version[1] != expected
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("Published build differs from tested build")
            print("Published HTML/API match tested build:", expected)
            return
        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
            if attempt + 1 == attempts:
                raise
            time.sleep(2**attempt)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--url", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--expected", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    verify(args.url, args.expected)

        run: |
          python -u -m scripts.sync_esstu

          EXPECTED_BUILD: ${{ steps.tested.outputs.build_id }}

          python -m scripts.build_static_site
          python -m scripts.publication --mode "$DATA_MODE" --baseline preview/data --expected-build "$EXPECTED_BUILD"
          python -m scripts.smoke_site

        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
        run: python -m scripts.data_branch save
      - name: Diagnostic artifacts
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2 (Node 24)

          name: diagnostics-${{ github.run_id }}
          path: |
            demo_data/*sync.json
            dist/api/status.json
            dist/api/sources.json
            dist/api/catalog-status.json
            test-results/
          if-no-files-found: ignore
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2 (Node 24)
        if: inputs.sync_sources == true
        with:
          name: source-snapshot
          path: demo_data
          retention-days: 14
      - uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@fc324d3547104276b827a68afc52ff2a11cc49c9 # v5.0.0 (Node 24)
        if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' && (github.event_name == 'push' || inputs.publish == true)
        with:
          path: dist

    needs: check
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' && (github.event_name == 'push' || inputs.publish == true)
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04

===== .github/workflows/sync.yml =====
SHA-256: 1f0b80bea8a81e13a93c6bd91e4116cc649e293fab9dbd4039c8ae418d41f20f

name: Scheduled source synchronization

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 */3 * * *'
    # 08:00–18:00 Asia/Irkutsk, Monday–Saturday, in UTC.
    - cron: '*/30 0-9 * * 1-6'
    - cron: '0 10 * * 1-6'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: source-synchronization
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  synchronize:
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    uses: ./.github/workflows/ci.yml
    with:
      sync_sources: true
      publish: true
    permissions:
      contents: write
      actions: read
      pages: write
      id-token: write

  notify:
    needs: synchronize
    if: always() && needs.synchronize.result != 'skipped'
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 5
    permissions:
      issues: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/github-script@3a2844b7e9c422d3c10d287c895573f7108da1b3 # v9.0.0 (Node 24)
        env:
          SYNC_RESULT: ${{ needs.synchronize.result }}
        with:
          script: |
            const title = 'Автоматическая синхронизация расписания: ошибка';
            const {owner,repo} = context.repo;
            const issues = await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo,{owner,repo,state:'open',creator:'github-actions[bot]'});
            const existing = issues.find(issue => !issue.pull_request && issue.title === title);
            const run = `https://github.com/${owner}/${repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}`;
            if (['failure','cancelled'].includes(process.env.SYNC_RESULT)) {
              const body = `Синхронизация или проверка новой выгрузки завершилась ошибкой или была отменена. Предыдущая опубликованная версия сохранена.\n\n[Журнал и диагностические файлы](${run}).`;
              if (existing) await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,body});
              else await github.rest.issues.create({owner,repo,title,body});
            } else if (existing) {
              await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,state:'closed',body:`Синхронизация и публикация восстановились. [Успешный запуск](${run}).`});
            }

SHA-256: 048b9a8d271c3ad49595f2d11123ab31a1719b48c7756fc3f73e3eb1aac0965d
