TEACHERS_META_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "teachers_meta.json"
LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)
META_LOCK = RLock()
AVATAR_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "static"


def local_teacher_photo(value: str) -> str:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or len(value) > 128
        or not re.fullmatch(
            r"avatars/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.(?:png|jpe?g|webp)", value, re.IGNORECASE
        )
    ):
        return ""
    path = (AVATAR_ROOT / value).resolve()
    return (
        value if path.is_relative_to(AVATAR_ROOT.resolve()) and path.is_file() else ""
    )


def repair_mojibake(text: str) -> str:
    def repair(match):
        word = match.group()
        if not re.search(r"[РС]", word):
            return word
        try:
            decoded = word.encode("cp1251").decode("utf-8")
        except (UnicodeError, ValueError):
            return word
        return (
            decoded
            if len(decoded) < len(word) and re.search("[А-Яа-яЁё]", decoded)
            else word
        )

    return re.sub(r"\S+", repair, text or "")


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", repair_mojibake(text)).strip()


def normalize_group_name(name: str) -> str:
    """Нормализует название группы для защиты от плавающих URL (Issue #4: 78.htm -> 80.htm)."""
    s = clean_text(name).lower().replace("ё", "е").rstrip(".")
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)


@lru_cache(maxsize=4)
def _read_teachers_meta(path: str, modified_ns: int, size: int):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def load_teachers_meta() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        stat = TEACHERS_META_PATH.stat()
        return deepcopy(
            _read_teachers_meta(str(TEACHERS_META_PATH), stat.st_mtime_ns, stat.st_size)
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return {}
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        LOGGER.warning("Could not read teacher metadata", exc_info=True)
        return {}


def save_teacher_meta_entry(teacher_key: str, entry: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if entry.get("photo") and not local_teacher_photo(entry["photo"]):
        raise ValueError(
            "Фото должно быть существующим локальным файлом avatars/*.png, *.jpg или *.webp"
        )
    with META_LOCK:
        meta = load_teachers_meta()
        norm_key = normalize_teacher_key(teacher_key)
        existing = meta.get(norm_key, {})
        existing.update(entry)
        meta[norm_key] = existing
        TEACHERS_META_PATH.write_text(
            json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        _read_teachers_meta.cache_clear()
        return existing


def normalize_teacher_key(raw_name: str) -> str:
    cleaned = clean_text(raw_name).lower().replace("ё", "е")
    m = re.search(r"([а-яa-z\-]+)\s+([а-яa-z])\.\s*([а-яa-z])\.", cleaned)
    if m:
        return f"{m.group(1)} {m.group(2)}.{m.group(3)}."
    return cleaned


def format_teacher_display_name(raw_name: str) -> str:
    cleaned = clean_text(raw_name)
    m = re.search(r"([А-ЯЁа-яё\-]+)\s+([А-ЯЁа-яё])\.\s*([А-ЯЁа-яё])\.", cleaned)
    if m:
        surname = "-".join(p.capitalize() for p in m.group(1).split("-"))
        return f"{surname} {m.group(2).upper()}.{m.group(3).upper()}."
    return cleaned


def build_teacher_profile(
    raw_name: str, caf_id: str = "", department_name: str = ""
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    norm_key = normalize_teacher_key(raw_name)
    display_name = format_teacher_display_name(raw_name)
    meta = load_teachers_meta().get(norm_key, {})
    parts = display_name.replace(".", " ").split()
    initials = "".join(p[0].upper() for p in parts[:2]) if parts else "П"
    return {
        "key": norm_key,
        "raw_name": clean_text(raw_name),
        "short_name": meta.get("short_name") or display_name,
        "full_name": meta.get("full_name") or display_name,
        "degree": meta.get("degree") or "Преподаватель ВСГУТУ",
        "department": meta.get("department") or department_name or "Кафедра ВСГУТУ",
        "caf_id": caf_id or meta.get("caf_id") or "",
        "building": meta.get("building") or "",
        "photo": local_teacher_photo(meta.get("photo")),
        "initials": initials,
    }


def get_reference_date(target_dt: datetime) -> datetime:
    year = target_dt.year
    ref_year = year - 1 if target_dt.month <= 7 else year
    first_sept = datetime(ref_year, 9, 1, tzinfo=target_dt.tzinfo)
    wd = first_sept.weekday()
    if wd == 0:
        return datetime(ref_year, 9, 1, tzinfo=target_dt.tzinfo)
    elif wd in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        return datetime(ref_year, 8, 32 - wd, tzinfo=target_dt.tzinfo)
    elif wd == 5:
        return datetime(ref_year, 9, 3, tzinfo=target_dt.tzinfo)
    return datetime(ref_year, 9, 2, tzinfo=target_dt.tzinfo)


def get_week_num(target_dt: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    ulan_ude_tz = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
    if target_dt is None:
        target_dt = datetime.now(ulan_ude_tz)
    elif target_dt.tzinfo is None:
        target_dt = target_dt.replace(tzinfo=ulan_ude_tz)
    else:
        target_dt = target_dt.astimezone(ulan_ude_tz)
    ref_date = get_reference_date(target_dt)
    diff_weeks = (target_dt - ref_date).total_seconds() / (60 * 60 * 24 * 7)
    is_odd = (
        (int(diff_weeks) % 2 == 0)
        if diff_weeks >= 0
        else (int(diff_weeks + 1 / 7) % 2 != 0)
    )
    return {
        "week_number": 1 if is_odd else 2,
        "week_title": "I неделя" if is_odd else "II неделя",
        "reference_date": ref_date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
    }


def extract_tags(text: str) -> list[str]:
    tags = []
    low = text.lower()
    for lang in ("англ", "франц", "нем", "кит"):
        if lang in low:
            tags.append(lang)
    for subgroup in ("1", "2"):
        if re.search(
            r"(?<!\d)"
            + subgroup
            + r"\s*(?:[-–]\s*[яй]\s*)?(?:п\s*/?\s*гр?\.?|подгрупп[а-яё]*|подгр\.?)(?![а-яё\d])",
            low,
        ):
            tags.append(f"{subgroup} п/г")
    return tags


def parse_single_lesson_chunk(chunk: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    raw = clean_text(chunk)
    lesson_type, type_code, rest = "Занятие", "other", raw
    type_match = re.match(
        r"^(лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|сем\.|конс\.|экз\.|зач\.)\s*(.*)$",
        raw,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    if type_match:
        prefix = type_match.group(1).lower()
        rest = type_match.group(2).strip()
        if prefix.startswith("лек"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Лекция", "lecture"
        elif prefix.startswith("пр") or prefix.startswith("сем"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Практика", "practice"
        elif prefix.startswith("лаб"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Лабораторная", "lab"
        elif prefix.startswith("экз"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Экзамен", "exam"
        elif prefix.startswith("зач"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Зачёт", "test"
        elif prefix.startswith("конс"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Консультация", "consultation"
    elif "физическая культура" in raw.lower() or "фкс" in raw.lower():
        lesson_type, type_code = "Физкультура", "sport"

    room = ""
    room_match = re.search(r"\bа\.?\s*([0-9А-Яа-яA-Za-z\-/]+(?:\s+и/[пд])?)\s*$", rest)
    if room_match:
        room = room_match.group(1).strip()
        rest = rest[: room_match.start()].strip()

    teacher, teacher_key, teacher_profile = "", "", None
    teacher_match = re.search(
        r"([А-ЯЁ][А-ЯЁа-яё\-]+\s+[А-ЯЁ]\.\s*[А-ЯЁ]\.(?:[А-ЯЁа-яё\.]+)?)\s*[-–—]?\s*$",
        rest,
    )
    if teacher_match:
        raw_t = teacher_match.group(1).strip()
        teacher = format_teacher_display_name(raw_t)
        teacher_key = normalize_teacher_key(raw_t)
        teacher_profile = build_teacher_profile(raw_t)
        rest = rest[: teacher_match.start()].strip()

    tags = extract_tags(raw)
    ambiguous = len(re.findall(r"\bа\.\s*\d", raw, re.IGNORECASE)) > 1 or room.endswith(
        ("-", "/")
    )
    if ambiguous:
        # Keep the original cell for inspection instead of assigning one teacher/room to several lessons.
        rest, lesson_type, type_code = (
            "Несколько занятий — сверьте с источником",
            "Требуется сверка",
            "other",
        )
        room, teacher, teacher_key, teacher_profile = "", "", "", None
    missing_subject = bool(teacher_match) and not rest
    warning = ""
    if ambiguous:
        warning = "В ячейке несколько аудиторий или неполный номер. Проверьте исходное расписание."
    elif missing_subject:
        rest, lesson_type = "Предмет не указан", "Требуется сверка"
        warning = "В ячейке указано только ФИО преподавателя. Название предмета отсутствует — сверьте с источником."
    elif re.search(r"\bа\.\s*\d|[А-ЯЁ][А-ЯЁа-яё-]+\s+[А-ЯЁ]\.\s*[А-ЯЁ]\.", rest):
        rest, lesson_type, type_code = (
            "Не удалось разобрать занятие — сверьте с источником",
            "Требуется сверка",
            "other",
        )
        warning = "В названии остались ФИО или обозначение аудитории. Проверьте исходную ячейку."
    return {
        "raw": raw,
        "type": lesson_type,
        "type_code": type_code,
        "subject": rest if rest else raw,
        "teacher": teacher,
        "teacher_key": teacher_key,
        "teacher_photo": teacher_profile["photo"] if teacher_profile else "",
        "teacher_caf_id": teacher_profile["caf_id"] if teacher_profile else "",
        "room": room,
        "tag": ", ".join(tags),
        "tags": tags,
        "groups": "",
        "parse_warning": warning,
        **({"needs_review": True} if warning else {}),
    }


def parse_cell_lessons(cell_text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    text = clean_text(cell_text)
    if not text or text in ("_", "-", "—"):
        return []
    parts = re.split(
        r"(?=(?:^|\s)(?:лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|сем\.|экз\.|зач\.|конс\.))",
        text,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    parts = [clean_text(p) for p in parts if clean_text(p) and clean_text(p) != "_"]
    expanded_parts: list[str] = []
    base_prefix = ""
    for p in parts:
        m_pref = re.match(
            r"^(лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|сем\.)\s*([А-ЯЁа-яё\s]+?)(?=\(|[А-ЯЁ]{2,})",
            p,
            flags=re.IGNORECASE,
        )
        if m_pref:
            base_prefix = f"{m_pref.group(1)}{m_pref.group(2).strip()} "
        marked = re.sub(
            r"(\bа\.[0-9А-Яа-яA-Za-z\-/]{2,10})\s+(?=(?:лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|\(|[А-ЯЁ]))",
            r"\1|||SPLIT|||",
            p,
        )
        for sc in marked.split("|||SPLIT|||"):
            sc_clean = clean_text(sc)
            if not sc_clean:
                continue
            if base_prefix and not re.match(
                r"^(лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|сем\.)", sc_clean, flags=re.IGNORECASE
            ):
                if sc_clean.startswith("("):
                    sc_clean = base_prefix + sc_clean
            expanded_parts.append(sc_clean)
    final_parts = expanded_parts if expanded_parts else [text]
    return [parse_single_lesson_chunk(p) for p in final_parts]


def parse_teacher_cell_lessons(
    cell_text: str, teacher_name: str = "", section: str = "bakalavriat"
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    text = clean_text(cell_text)
    if not text or text in ("_", "-", "—"):
        return []
    lesson_type, type_code, subject, before_subject, groups_str, room = (
        "Занятие",
        "other",
        text,
        text,
        "",
        "",
    )
    type_match = re.search(
        r"\b(лек\.|пр\.|лаб\.|сем\.|экз\.|зач\.|конс\.)\s*(.+)$",
        text,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    if type_match:
        prefix = type_match.group(1).lower()
        subject = clean_text(type_match.group(2))
        before_subject = clean_text(text[: type_match.start()])
        if prefix.startswith("лек"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Лекция", "lecture"
        elif prefix.startswith("пр") or prefix.startswith("сем"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Практика", "practice"
        elif prefix.startswith("лаб"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Лабораторная", "lab"
        elif prefix.startswith("экз"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Экзамен", "exam"
        elif prefix.startswith("зач"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Зачёт", "test"
        elif prefix.startswith("конс"):
            lesson_type, type_code = "Консультация", "consultation"
    room_match = re.search(
        r"\bа\.\s*((?:[0-9][0-9А-Яа-яA-Za-z\-/]*|дист\.*|д\.о\.[/]*)(?:\s+и/[пд])?)",
        before_subject,
        flags=re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    if room_match:
        room = room_match.group(1).strip()
        groups_str = clean_text(before_subject[: room_match.start()])
        if not type_match:
            subject = (
                clean_text(before_subject[room_match.end() :])
                or "Предмет не указан в источнике"
            )
    else:
        groups_str = before_subject
    tags = extract_tags(text)
    return [
        {
            "raw": text,
            "type": lesson_type,
            "type_code": type_code,
            "subject": subject,
            "teacher": format_teacher_display_name(teacher_name)
            if teacher_name
            else "",
            "teacher_key": normalize_teacher_key(teacher_name) if teacher_name else "",
            "room": room,
            "groups": groups_str,
            "section": section,
            "section_badge": "Магистратура/Колледж"
            if section == "spezialitet"
            else "Бакалавриат",
            "tag": ", ".join(tags),
            "tags": tags,
        }
    ]


def parse_day_or_date_cell(
    raw_cell_text: str, fallback_row_idx: int = 0
) -> tuple[str, str, str, int, str] | None:
    """Поддержка стандартных дней ('Пнд'..'Сбт') и дат сессии заочников ('15 янв', '16.01')."""
    cleaned = clean_text(raw_cell_text).lower()
    if not cleaned:
        return None
    prefix3 = cleaned[:3]

    m_ru = re.search(r"(\d{1,2})\s*([а-яё]{3,})(?:\s+(\d{4}))?", cleaned)
    if m_ru and m_ru.group(2)[:3] in RU_MONTHS:
        day_num = int(m_ru.group(1))
        mon_num = RU_MONTHS[m_ru.group(2)[:3]]
        year = int(m_ru.group(3)) if m_ru.group(3) else None
        date_label = f"{day_num:02d}.{mon_num:02d}" + (f".{year:04d}" if year else "")
        # A date without a year does not identify a weekday. Keep it as a dated row.
        try:
            datetime(year or 2000, mon_num, day_num)
        except ValueError:
            return None
        return (
            f"date_{date_label}",
            date_label,
            date_label,
            DAYS_MAP.get(prefix3, ("", "", "", -1))[3],
            date_label,
        )

    m_num = re.search(r"(\d{1,2})\.(\d{1,2})(?:\.(\d{4}))?", cleaned)
    if m_num:
        year = int(m_num.group(3)) if m_num.group(3) else None
        date_label = f"{int(m_num.group(1)):02d}.{int(m_num.group(2)):02d}" + (
            f".{year:04d}" if year else ""
        )
        try:
            datetime(year or 2000, int(m_num.group(2)), int(m_num.group(1)))
        except ValueError:
            return None
        return (
            f"date_{date_label}",
            date_label,
            date_label,
            DAYS_MAP.get(prefix3, ("", "", "", -1))[3],
            date_label,
        )
    if prefix3 in DAYS_MAP:
        day_key, day_title, day_short, day_index = DAYS_MAP[prefix3]
        return day_key, day_title, day_short, day_index, ""
    return None


def parse_groups_list_html(
    html: str, section: str = "bakalavriat"
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
    groups, seen = [], set()
    table = soup.find("table")
    if not table:
        return groups
    rows = table.find_all("tr")
    if not rows:
        return groups
    courses = []
    for td in rows[0].find_all(["td", "th"]):
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)", clean_text(td.get_text()))
        courses.append(int(m.group(1)) if m else len(courses) + 1)
    for tr in rows[1:]:
        for col_idx, td in enumerate(tr.find_all("td")):
            course = courses[col_idx] if col_idx < len(courses) else col_idx + 1
            for a in td.find_all("a"):
                href = (a.get("href") or "").strip()
                name = clean_text(a.get_text())
                if not name or not href or (section, href, name) in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add((section, href, name))
                groups.append(
                    {
                        "id": href.replace(".htm", ""),
                        "href": href,
                        "name": name,
                        "norm_name": normalize_group_name(name),
                        "course": course,
                        "section": section,
                        "section_title": SECTIONS.get(section, section),
                    }
                )
    return groups


class ScheduleStructureError(ValueError):
    """Source table cannot be assigned to weeks without guessing."""


def parse_14row_table(table, is_teacher=False, owner_name="", section="bakalavriat"):
    rows = table.find_all("tr", recursive=False) if table else []
    if not rows and table:
        rows = table.select(":scope > tbody > tr")
    if len(rows) < 3:
        raise ScheduleStructureError("Нет заголовков пар, времени или строк занятий")

    def cells(row):
        return row.find_all(["td", "th"], recursive=False)

    headers, times = cells(rows[0]), cells(rows[1])
    if len(headers) != len(times) or len(headers) < 2:
        raise ScheduleStructureError("Не совпадают столбцы заголовков и времени")
    bells, columns = [], []
    data_rows = []
    explicit_week = None
    for row in rows[2:]:
        tds = cells(row)
        if not tds or not clean_text(row.get_text(" ")):
            continue
        text = clean_text(row.get_text(" ")).lower()
        heading = re.fullmatch(
            r"(i{1,2}|[12])\s*недел[яи]\s*(?:\((?:не)?ч[её]тная\))?", text
        )
        if heading and len(tds) == 1:
            explicit_week = "1" if heading[1] in ("i", "1") else "2"
            continue
        if len(tds) != len(headers):
            raise ScheduleStructureError("Число ячеек строки не совпадает с заголовком")
        day = parse_day_or_date_cell(tds[0].get_text())
        if not day:
            raise ScheduleStructureError(
                "Не распознан день или дата: " + clean_text(tds[0].get_text())
            )
        data_rows.append((tds, day, explicit_week))
    if not data_rows:
        raise ScheduleStructureError("Нет распознанных дней занятий")
    for column in range(1, len(headers)):
        title, time = (
            clean_text(headers[column].get_text()),
            clean_text(times[column].get_text()),
        )
        content = any(
            clean_text(row[0][column].get_text()) not in ("", "_", "-")
            for row in data_rows
        )
        if not time and not content:
            continue  # Portal exports blank padding, including an unused eighth pair.
        match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{1,2}:\d{2})\s*[-–]\s*(\d{1,2}:\d{2})", time)
        number = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+)(?:-?я)?", title)
        if not match or not number or int(number[1]) != len(bells) + 1:
            raise ScheduleStructureError(
                "Не распознан номер или интервал пары: " + title + " / " + time
            )
        start, end = (value.zfill(5) for value in match.groups())
        if (
            not all(
                re.fullmatch(r"(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d", value)
                for value in (start, end)
            )
            or start >= end
        ):
            raise ScheduleStructureError("Некорректное время пары")
        bells.append(
            {
                "number": int(number[1]),
                "label": f"{number[1]}-я",
                "start": start,
                "end": end,
            }
        )
        columns.append(column)
    if not bells:
        raise ScheduleStructureError("Нет интервалов звонков")
    dated_only = all(day[4] for _, day, _ in data_rows)
    explicit = all(week for _, _, week in data_rows)
    # Exported portal format: two complete ordered Mon-Sat blocks. Font colour
    # is presentation only. Unknown/truncated/mixed layouts fail explicitly.
    structural = (
        len(data_rows) == 12
        and [day[3] for _, day, _ in data_rows] == list(range(6)) * 2
    )
    if not explicit and not dated_only and not structural:
        raise ScheduleStructureError(
            "Ожидались два полных блока Пн–Сб или явные заголовки недель"
        )
    if not explicit and any(week for _, _, week in data_rows):
        if not structural or any(
            week and week != str(index // 6 + 1)
            for index, (_, _, week) in enumerate(data_rows)
        ):
            raise ScheduleStructureError(
                "Заголовки недель противоречат структуре таблицы"
            )
    weeks, total = {"1": {}, "2": {}}, 0
    for index, (tds, day, heading_week) in enumerate(data_rows):
        key, title, short, day_index, date = day
        week = (
            heading_week if explicit else ("1" if dated_only else str(index // 6 + 1))
        )
        if key in weeks[week]:
            raise ScheduleStructureError(f"Повтор дня/даты: {week}/{key}")
        pairs = []
        for bell, column in zip(bells, columns):
            text = tds[column].get_text(" ")
            lessons = (
                parse_teacher_cell_lessons(text, owner_name, section)
                if is_teacher
                else parse_cell_lessons(text)
            )
            empty = clean_text(text) in ("", "_", "-", "—")
            review = any(
                lesson.get("parse_warning")
                or lesson.get("subject") == "Предмет не указан в источнике"
                for lesson in lessons
            )
            record_cell(empty=empty, lost=not empty and not lessons, review=review)
            total += len(lessons)
            pairs.append({**bell, "is_empty": not lessons, "lessons": lessons})
        weeks[week][key] = {
            "day_key": key,
            "day_title": title,
            "day_short": short,
            "day_index": day_index,
            "session_date": date,
            "pairs": pairs,
        }
    return {
        "bells": bells,
        "weeks": weeks,
        "total_lessons": total,
        "is_incomplete": False,
        "parse_errors": [],
    }


def parse_table_with_status(table, source, **kwargs):
    try:
        return parse_14row_table(table, **kwargs)
    except ScheduleStructureError as error:
        record_error(source, str(error))
        return {
            "bells": [],
            "weeks": {"1": {}, "2": {}},
            "total_lessons": 0,
            "is_incomplete": True,
            "parse_errors": [str(error)],
        }


def parse_group_schedule_html(
    html: str, group_id: str = "1", section: str = "bakalavriat"
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
    # The semantic heading identifies the owner; font colour is presentation.
    prefix = []
    for node in soup.descendants:
        if getattr(node, "name", None) == "table":
            break
        if isinstance(node, str):
            prefix.append(str(node))
    heading = re.search(
        r"Расписание\s+занятий\s+учебной\s+группы\s*:\s*(.+)$",
        clean_text(" ".join(prefix)),
        re.I,
    )
    group_name = clean_text(heading.group(1)) if heading else ""
    if not group_name:
        font = soup.find("font", attrs={"color": re.compile(r"#ff00ff", re.I)})
        group_name = clean_text(font.get_text()) if font else group_id
    parsed = parse_table_with_status(
        soup.find("table"),
        f"{section}/{group_id}.htm",
        is_teacher=False,
        owner_name=group_name,
        section=section,
    )
    source_file = f"{section}/{group_id}.htm"
    for days in parsed["weeks"].values():
        for day in days.values():
            for pair in day["pairs"]:
                for lesson in pair["lessons"]:
                    lesson.update(source_type="group_table", source_file=source_file)
    return {
        "mode": "student",
        "source_file": source_file,
        "source_type": "group_table",
        "group_id": group_id,
        "group_name": group_name,
        "norm_group_name": normalize_group_name(group_name),
        "section": section,
        "section_title": SECTIONS.get(section, section),
        "is_incomplete": parsed["is_incomplete"],
        "parse_errors": parsed["parse_errors"],
        "bells": parsed["bells"],
        "weeks": parsed["weeks"],
        "total_lessons": parsed["total_lessons"],
    }


def parse_departments_list_html(
    html: str, section: str = "bakalavriat"
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
    institutes = []
    for div in soup.find_all("div", class_="faculty"):
        h2 = div.find(["h2", "h3"])
        inst_title = (
            clean_text(h2.get_text()) if h2 else "Общеуниверситетские подразделения"
        )
        depts = []
        for a in div.find_all("a"):
            href, name = (a.get("href") or "").strip(), clean_text(a.get_text())
            if href and name:
                depts.append(
                    {
                        "caf_id": href.replace(".htm", ""),
                        "href": href,
                        "name": name,
                        "institute": inst_title,
                        "section": section,
                    }
                )
        if depts:
            institutes.append({"institute": inst_title, "departments": depts})
    return institutes


def parse_department_teachers_html(
    html: str,
    caf_id: str = "Caf5",
    section: str = "bakalavriat",
    department_name: str = "",
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
    teachers_list = []
    for idx, table in enumerate(soup.find_all("table")):
        prev_font = table.find_previous(
            "font", attrs={"color": re.compile(r"#ff00ff", re.IGNORECASE)}
        )
        raw_name = clean_text(prev_font.get_text()) if prev_font else ""
        previous_table = table.find_previous("table")
        if not raw_name or (
            previous_table is not None
            and prev_font.find_previous("table") is not previous_table
        ):
            record_error(
                f"{section}/{caf_id}.htm", f"Teacher heading missing: table {idx + 1}"
            )
            continue
        profile = build_teacher_profile(
            raw_name, caf_id=caf_id, department_name=department_name
        )
        parsed = parse_table_with_status(
            table,
            f"{section}/{caf_id}.htm#{raw_name}",
            is_teacher=True,
            owner_name=raw_name,
            section=section,
        )
        source_file = f"{section}/{caf_id}.htm"
        profile["department"] = department_name or profile["department"]
        for days in parsed["weeks"].values():
            for day in days.values():
                for pair in day["pairs"]:
                    for lesson in pair["lessons"]:
                        lesson.update(
                            source_type="official_caf", source_file=source_file
                        )
        teachers_list.append(
            {
                "mode": "teacher",
                "source_file": source_file,
                "source_type": "official_caf",
                "teacher_key": profile["key"],
                "teacher_name": profile["short_name"],
                "profile": profile,
                "caf_id": caf_id,
                "section": section,
                "is_incomplete": parsed["is_incomplete"],
                "parse_errors": parsed["parse_errors"],
                "bells": parsed["bells"],
                "weeks": parsed["weeks"],
            }
        )
    return teachers_list


def merge_teacher_schedules(
    primary: dict[str, Any], secondary: dict[str, Any] | None
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not secondary:
        return primary
    merged = json.loads(json.dumps(primary))
    merged["is_incomplete"] = bool(
        primary.get("is_incomplete") or secondary.get("is_incomplete")
    )
    merged["parse_errors"] = primary.get("parse_errors", []) + secondary.get(
        "parse_errors", []
    )
    merged["merged_sections"] = list(
        {primary.get("section", "bakalavriat"), secondary.get("section", "spezialitet")}
    )
    for week_key in ("1", "2"):
        sec_week = secondary.get("weeks", {}).get(week_key, {})
        prim_week = merged.setdefault("weeks", {}).setdefault(week_key, {})
        for day_key, sec_day in sec_week.items():
            if day_key not in prim_week:
                prim_week[day_key] = deepcopy(sec_day)
                continue
            prim_pairs = prim_week[day_key].get("pairs", [])
            sec_pairs = sec_day.get("pairs", [])
            for pair in sec_pairs:
                target = next(
                    (
                        existing
                        for existing in prim_pairs
                        if (existing["number"], existing["start"], existing["end"])
                        == (pair["number"], pair["start"], pair["end"])
                    ),
                    None,
                )
                if target is None:
                    target = {**deepcopy(pair), "lessons": []}
                    prim_pairs.append(target)
                existing_raws = {
                    (
                        lesson.get("raw"),
                        lesson.get("source_file"),
                        lesson.get("subject"),
                        lesson.get("groups"),
                        lesson.get("room"),
                    )
                    for lesson in target["lessons"]
                }
                for lesson in pair.get("lessons", []):
                    key = (
                        lesson.get("raw"),
                        lesson.get("source_file"),
                        lesson.get("subject"),
                        lesson.get("groups"),
                        lesson.get("room"),
                    )
                    if key not in existing_raws:
                        target["lessons"].append(deepcopy(lesson))
                        existing_raws.add(key)
                target["is_empty"] = not target["lessons"]
            prim_pairs.sort(key=lambda pair: (pair["start"], pair["number"]))
    return merged


===== esstu_schedule/data/repository.py =====
SHA-256: e19cc55f53e1db2c5be2d89e2704c42b848a9d9ac8e55b7fde83b01367e5b2c4

"""One validated parsing boundary for the dynamic service and static builder."""

from ..parser import parse_department_teachers_html, parse_group_schedule_html
from .models import validate_schedule


def group_schedule(html, group_id="1", section="bakalavriat"):
    return validate_schedule(parse_group_schedule_html(html, group_id, section))


def department_schedules(
    html, caf_id="Caf5", section="bakalavriat", department_name=""
):
    return [
        validate_schedule(schedule)
        for schedule in parse_department_teachers_html(
            html, caf_id, section, department_name
        )
    ]


def group_identity_error(schedule, expected_name):
    from ..parser import normalize_group_name

    actual = schedule.get("group_name", "")
    if (
        not expected_name
        or not any(character.isalnum() for character in expected_name)
        or expected_name == "Магистратура"
    ):
        return "catalog_placeholder"
    if actual == schedule.get("group_id"):
        return "heading_missing"
    if normalize_group_name(actual) != normalize_group_name(expected_name):
        return "group_name_mismatch"
    return None


===== esstu_schedule/data/diagnostics.py =====
SHA-256: 18238c25cb91b822c38508ddf36e43164fa5cd8751e09f20ace99342376729fc

"""Bounded, thread-safe diagnostics shared by parsing, HTTP and static builds."""

import logging
from collections import Counter, deque
from threading import RLock

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("esstu_schedule.data")
_lock, _counts, _recent = RLock(), Counter(), deque(maxlen=50)
_unparsed_cells = 0
_cells = Counter()


def record_cell(*, empty=False, lost=False, review=False):
    global _unparsed_cells
    with _lock:
        _cells["empty_cell_count"] += int(empty)
        _cells["lost_cell_count"] += int(lost)
        _cells["review_cell_count"] += int(review)
        _unparsed_cells += int(
            review
        )  # Legacy field: reviewable cells, not lost cells.


def record_error(source, message):
    LOGGER.warning("Incomplete schedule %s: %s", source, message)
    with _lock:
        _counts[str(source)] += 1
        _recent.append({"source": str(source), "message": str(message)})


def parsing_status():
    with _lock:
        return {
            "parse_error_count": sum(_counts.values()),
            "failed_source_count": len(_counts),
            "parse_errors": list(_recent),
            "unparsed_cell_count": _unparsed_cells,
            **{
                key: _cells[key]
                for key in ("empty_cell_count", "lost_cell_count", "review_cell_count")
            },
        }


def reset_diagnostics():
    global _unparsed_cells
    with _lock:
        _counts.clear()
        _recent.clear()
        _unparsed_cells = 0
        _cells.clear()


===== esstu_schedule/rooms.py =====
SHA-256: d424d6a08600da4f4df2b3eb3f7ab6e8f734ecea5eb3ae0b9add9b4ed2dca552

"""Shared room index for the HTTP server and GitHub Pages snapshots."""

import re


def normalize_room(raw):
    return re.sub(
        r"\s+", " ", re.sub(r"\s*и/[пд]", "", str(raw or ""), flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    ).strip()


def build_rooms_index(schedules):
    rooms = {}
    for schedule in schedules:
        if schedule.get("is_incomplete"):
            continue
        profile = schedule.get("profile") or {}
        for week, days in (schedule.get("weeks") or {}).items():
            for day, data in days.items():
                for pair in data.get("pairs", []):
                    for lesson in pair.get("lessons", []):
                        room = normalize_room(lesson.get("room"))
                        if not room:
                            continue
                        slots = rooms.setdefault(room, {"room": room, "slots": {}})[
                            "slots"
                        ]
                        entries = slots.setdefault(f"{week}:{day}:{pair['number']}", [])
                        entry = {
                            key: lesson.get(key, "") for key in ("subject", "type")
                        }
                        entry.update(
                            pair=pair["number"],
                            start=pair["start"],
                            end=pair["end"],
                            teacher=lesson.get("teacher")
                            or profile.get("short_name", ""),
                            groups=lesson.get("groups")
                            or schedule.get("group_name", ""),
                        )
                        if entry not in entries:
                            entries.append(entry)
    for room in rooms.values():
        room["slots"] = dict(
            sorted(
                room["slots"].items(),
                key=lambda item: (
                    item[0].rsplit(":", 1)[0],
                    int(item[0].rsplit(":", 1)[1]),
                ),
            )
        )
        for entries in room["slots"].values():
            entries.sort(
                key=lambda entry: tuple(
                    str(entry.get(key, ""))
                    for key in ("start", "end", "subject", "teacher", "groups")
                )
            )
    result = sorted(rooms.values(), key=lambda item: item["room"])
    return {"count": len(result), "rooms": result, "coverage": "known_schedules"}

<script>
    // =========================================================================
    // 1. АВТО-РАСШИФРОВКА КОРПУСОВ И АУДИТОРИЙ ВСГУТУ (ФИШКА ВАРИАНТА 3)
    // =========================================================================
    function decodeEsstuRoom(rawRoom) {
      if (!rawRoom || !String(rawRoom).trim()) return null;
      const raw = String(rawRoom).trim(), board = /и\/д/i.test(raw);
      const clean = raw.replace(/\s*и\/[пд]/ig, '').trim();
      if(/^(?:online|онлайн|дист\.?|д\.о\.?)$/i.test(clean))return {raw,badge:'Онлайн',building:null,location:'Онлайн-занятие',online:true};
      const dash = clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})-(?:\d{2,4}[а-яa-z]?|спорт\d*)$/i);
      const zero = clean.match(/^0([1-9])\d{2}[а-яa-z]?$/i);
      const four = clean.match(/^([1-9])\d{3}[а-яa-z]?$/i);
      // Building prefixes describe the room notation; no floor or street is inferred.
      const id = dash?.[1] || zero?.[1] || four?.[1];
      const building = /вуц/i.test(clean) ? 'ВУЦ' : id ? `Корпус ${Number(id)}` : null;
      return {raw, badge:`ауд. ${raw}`, building,
        location:[building ? `${building} (по обозначению аудитории)` : '', board ? 'Интерактивная доска' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' • ')};
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. ОФИЦИАНАЯ ФОРМУЛА РАСЧЁТА I / II НЕДЕЛИ ВСГУТУ (из menu.htm)
    // =========================================================================
    function getReferenceDate(targetDateTime = undefined) {
      const currentDateTime = targetDateTime === undefined ? new Date() : targetDateTime;
      const currentYear = currentDateTime.getFullYear();
      const currentMonth = currentDateTime.getMonth();
      const referenceYear = currentMonth <= 6 ? currentYear - 1 : currentYear;

      const firstSeptember = new Date(referenceYear, 8, 1);
      const firstSeptemberDayOfWeek = firstSeptember.getDay();

      let referenceMonth = 8;
      let referenceDate = 1;
      switch (firstSeptemberDayOfWeek) {
        case 1: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 1; break;
        case 2: case 3: case 4: case 5:
          referenceMonth = 7; referenceDate = 31 - firstSeptemberDayOfWeek + 2; break;
        case 6: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 3; break;
        case 0: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 2; break;
      }
      return new Date(referenceYear, referenceMonth, referenceDate);
    }

    function getWeekNum(targetDateTime = undefined) {
      const currentDateTime = targetDateTime === undefined ? new Date() : targetDateTime;
      const referenceDate = getReferenceDate(currentDateTime);
      const calendarMillis = date => Date.UTC(date.getFullYear(),date.getMonth(),date.getDate(),date.getHours(),date.getMinutes(),date.getSeconds());
      const diff = (calendarMillis(currentDateTime) - calendarMillis(referenceDate)) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 7);
      return diff >= 0
        ? (Math.trunc(diff) % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 2)
        : (Math.trunc(diff + 1 / 7) % 2 === 0 ? 2 : 1);
    }

    let simOverride = null;

    function getUlanUdeNow() {
      const now = new Date();
      const parts=Object.fromEntries(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Irkutsk',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(now).map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
      const uuDate = new Date(+parts.year,+parts.month-1,+parts.day,+parts.hour,+parts.minute,+parts.second);
      if (simOverride) {
        const currentDayIdx = (uuDate.getDay() + 6) % 7;
        uuDate.setDate(uuDate.getDate() + (simOverride.dayIndex - currentDayIdx));
        uuDate.setHours(simOverride.hours, simOverride.minutes, now.getSeconds(), 0);
      }
      return uuDate;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. ТЕМА ОФОРМЛЕНИЯ И СОСТОЯНИЕ
    // =========================================================================
    let themeMode = localStorage.getItem('esstu_theme') || 'dark';

    function applyTheme() {
      const root = document.documentElement;
      const btn = document.getElementById('themeToggleBtn');
      let effectiveDark = true;
      if (themeMode === 'dark') { effectiveDark = true; btn.textContent = 'Тёмная тема'; }
      else if (themeMode === 'light') { effectiveDark = false; btn.textContent = 'Светлая тема'; }
      else { effectiveDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches; btn.textContent = 'Системная тема'; }
      root.classList.toggle('dark', effectiveDark);
    }

    function cycleTheme() {
      const order = ['dark', 'light', 'system'];
      themeMode = order[(order.indexOf(themeMode) + 1) % order.length];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_theme', themeMode);
      applyTheme();
    }

    const DAY_KEYS = ['monday', 'tuesday', 'wednesday', 'thursday', 'friday', 'saturday'];
    const DAY_NAMES = [
      { key: 'monday', short: 'Пн', full: 'Понедельник' },
      { key: 'tuesday', short: 'Вт', full: 'Вторник' },
      { key: 'wednesday', short: 'Ср', full: 'Среда' },
      { key: 'thursday', short: 'Чт', full: 'Четверг' },
      { key: 'friday', short: 'Пт', full: 'Пятница' },
      { key: 'saturday', short: 'Сб', full: 'Суббота' },
    ];

    let appMode = localStorage.getItem('esstu_mode') || 'student';
    let viewMode = localStorage.getItem('esstu_view_mode') || 'day'; // 'day' | 'week'
    let allGroups = [];
    let departmentsHierarchy = [];
    let allTeachersDirectory = [];
    let selectedCourseFilter = 0;
    let currentSchedule = null;
    let selectedWeek = 1;
    let selectedDayKey = 'monday';

    let savedState = {
      section: localStorage.getItem('esstu_section') || 'bakalavriat',
      groupId: localStorage.getItem('esstu_group_id') || '1',
      groupName: localStorage.getItem('esstu_group_name') || '1146 тпуп',
      teacherKey: localStorage.getItem('esstu_teacher_key') || 'дарибазарон э.ч.',
      teacherCafId: localStorage.getItem('esstu_teacher_caf') || 'Caf5',
      hideEmpty: localStorage.getItem('esstu_hide_empty') !== 'false',
    };

    // Заметки
    function getNotesMap() {
      try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('esstu_notes_v1') || '{}'); }
      catch (e) { return {}; }
    }

    let editingNoteKey = null;

    function makeNoteKey(subject, pairNum, dayKey = selectedDayKey) {
      const owner = appMode === 'student' ? savedState.groupName : savedState.teacherKey;
      return `${owner}::${selectedWeek}::${dayKey}::${pairNum}::${subject}`;
    }

    function openNoteModal(noteKey, subjectTitle) {
      editingNoteKey = noteKey;
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      document.getElementById('noteModalTitle').textContent = `📝 Заметка: ${subjectTitle}`;
      document.getElementById('noteTextarea').value = notes[noteKey] || '';
      openSimpleDialog('noteModal');
      document.getElementById('noteTextarea').focus();
    }

    function closeNoteModal() {
      editingNoteKey = null;
      closeSimpleDialog('noteModal');
    }

    function saveCurrentNote() {
      if (!editingNoteKey) return;
      const val = document.getElementById('noteTextarea').value.trim();
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      if (val) notes[editingNoteKey] = val;
      else delete notes[editingNoteKey];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_notes_v1', JSON.stringify(notes));
      closeNoteModal();
      renderPairsList();
    }

    function deleteCurrentNote() {
      if (!editingNoteKey) return;
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      delete notes[editingNoteKey];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_notes_v1', JSON.stringify(notes));
      closeNoteModal();
      renderPairsList();
    }

    function filterLessons(lessons) { return (lessons || []).filter(lesson=>matchesSubgroup(lesson,activeSubgroup())); }
    function timeToMinutes(hhmm) {
      const [h, m] = hhmm.split(':').map(Number);
      return h * 60 + m;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. BENTO КАРТОЧКА РЕАЛЬНОГО ВРЕМЕНИ С КОЛЬЦЕВЫМ SVG-ТАЙМЕРОМ

    function updateLiveHero() { updateCampusHero(); }

    // =========================================================================
    // 5. ОТРИСОВКА ТАЙМЛАЙНА С ПЕРЕМЕНАМИ И СЕТКИ НА ВСЮ НЕДЕЛЮ
    // =========================================================================
    function renderHeaderAndNav() {
      const profileCard = document.getElementById('teacherProfileCard');

      if (appMode === 'student') {
        profileCard.classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent = `🎓 ${savedState.groupName}`;
      } else {
        if (currentSchedule && currentSchedule.profile) {
          const p = currentSchedule.profile;
          profileCard.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('teacherAvatarImg').src = teacherAvatarURL(p);
          document.getElementById('teacherAvatarImg').onerror = function() { this.onerror=null;this.src=initialsAvatar(p.short_name || p.full_name); };
          document.getElementById('teacherFullName').textContent = p.full_name || p.short_name;
          document.getElementById('teacherDegreeAndDept').textContent = `${p.degree} • ${escapeHTML(p.department)}`;
          document.getElementById('teacherBuildingInfo').textContent = (p.building ? `📍 ${p.building}` : '') + (currentSchedule.partial ? ' • По расписаниям групп: данные могут быть неполными' : '');
          document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent = `👨🏫 ${p.short_name}`;
        }
      }

      // Кнопки недель
      document.getElementById('btnWeek1').className = selectedWeek === 1
        ? 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';
      document.getElementById('btnWeek2').className = selectedWeek === 2
        ? 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';

      // Кнопки вида (День / Неделя)
      document.getElementById('btnViewDay').className = viewMode === 'day'
        ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';
      document.getElementById('btnViewWeek').className = viewMode === 'week'
        ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';

      document.getElementById('dayTabs').classList.toggle('hidden', viewMode === 'week');
    }

    function renderDayTabs() { renderCalendarDays(); }

    function getBadgeStyle(typeCode) {
      switch (typeCode) {
        case 'lecture':
          return 'bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 border-purple-500/20';
        case 'practice':
          return 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30';
        case 'lab':
          return 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border-amber-500/20';
        case 'sport':
          return 'bg-cyan-500/15 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/30';
        default:
          return 'bg-slate-500/15 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 border-slate-500/30';
      }
    }

    // Формирует плашку перемены между двумя парами
    function buildBreakIndicator(prevPair, nextPair) {
      if (!prevPair || !nextPair) return '';
      const endPrev = timeToMinutes(prevPair.end);
      const startNext = timeToMinutes(nextPair.start);
      const diffMin = startNext - endPrev;
      if (diffMin <= 0) return '';

      const previous = decodeEsstuRoom(prevPair.lessons?.[0]?.room);
      const next = decodeEsstuRoom(nextPair.lessons?.[0]?.room);
      let label = `☕ Перемена ${diffMin} мин`;
      const transfer = previous?.building && next?.building && previous.building !== next.building
        ? ` • 🏃 Смена корпуса: ${previous.building} → ${next.building}` : '';
      let style = 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#0b1524]';

      if (diffMin === 40) {
        label = `🍽 Большой обеденный перерыв • 40 мин (${prevPair.end}–${nextPair.start})`;
        style = 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5';
      } else if (diffMin > 60) {
        const hrs = Math.floor(diffMin / 60);
        const mins = diffMin % 60;
        label = `⏳ Свободное окно между парами • ${hrs} ч ${mins} мин (${prevPair.end}–${nextPair.start})`;
        style = 'text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 border-teal-500/30 bg-teal-500/5';
      }

      return `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 pl-6 py-0.5">
          <div class="w-0.5 h-4 bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800"></div>
          <span class="text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${style}">
            ${label}${transfer}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderPairsList() {
      const container = document.getElementById('pairsContainer');
      if (currentSchedule?.is_incomplete) { container.textContent='Данные неполные — расписание не удалось надёжно прочитать.'; return; }
      if (!currentSchedule) { container.textContent='Загрузка расписания…'; return; }
      if ((hasDatedSchedule(currentSchedule) || viewMode === 'dates') && scheduleTab !== 'session') { renderDatedTimetable(container); return; }
      if (scheduleTab === 'session') { renderSessionTimeline(container); return; }
      if (viewMode === 'week') { renderFullWeekGrid(container); return; }
      renderCompactDay(container);
    }

    function renderFullWeekGrid(container) {
      const weekObj = currentSchedule?.weeks?.[String(selectedWeek)] || {};
      const dayCards = DAY_NAMES.map((d) => {
        const dayData = weekObj[d.key];
        const activePairs = (dayData?.pairs || [])
          .map((p) => ({ ...p, filtered: filterLessons(p.lessons) }))
          .filter((p) => p.filtered.length > 0);

        const rowsHtml = groupOnlinePairs(activePairs).map((block) => {
          const p=activePairs[block.start],last=activePairs[block.end],count=block.end-block.start+1;
          const l = p.filtered[0];
          const extraCount = p.filtered.length > 1 ? ` (+${p.filtered.length - 1})` : '';
          return `
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 py-1.5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 last:border-0 text-xs">
              <div class="flex items-start gap-2 min-w-0">
                <span class="font-semibold text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 shrink-0">${count>1?`${p.start}–${last.end} · ${count} ${pairWord(count)}`:`${p.number} пара · ${p.start}`}</span>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                  <div class="font-bold truncate">${escapeHTML(displaySubject(l))}${extraCount}</div>
                  <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">${l.type} ${l.teacher ? '• ' + l.teacher : (l.groups || '')}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              ${lessonRoomLabel(l) ? `<span class="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">${escapeHTML(lessonRoomLabel(l))}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');

        return `
          <div class="rounded-3xl p-4 bg-white dark:bg-[#101c2e] border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <button onclick="selectDayAndSwitch('${d.key}')" class="font-extrabold text-sm text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 hover:underline">
                ${dayData?.day_title || d.full} →
              </button>
              <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">${activePairs.length} ${pairWord(activePairs.length)}</span>
            </div>
            ${rowsHtml || '<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic py-2">Выходной / пар нет</div>'}
          </div>
        `;
      });

      container.innerHTML = `<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">${dayCards.join('')}</div>`;
    }

    function selectDayAndSwitch(dayKey) {
      selectedDayKey = dayKey;
      setViewMode('day');
    }

    function setViewMode(mode) {
      scheduleTab = 'semester';
      viewMode = mode;
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_view_mode', mode);
      renderAll();
    }

    function bottomNavToday() {
      scheduleTab = 'semester';
      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';
      viewMode = 'day';
      renderAll();
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }

    function bottomNavToggleWeekView() {
      setViewMode(viewMode === 'day' ? 'week' : 'day');
    }

    function switchModeAndOpen(targetMode) {
      if (targetMode === 'student') {
        toggleGroupModal(true);
      } else {
        toggleTeacherModal(true);
      }
    }

    function renderAll() {
      renderFavoritesBar();
      renderHeaderAndNav();
      renderDayTabs();
      renderPairsList();
      updateLiveHero();
      updateExperienceUI();
      updateCompactUI();
      updateStudyUI();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 6. ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ И МОДАЛЬНЫЕ ОКНА (Поддержка Localhost + GitHub Pages)
    // =========================================================================
    function getApiUrl(path) {
      const isGhPages = window.location.hostname.includes('github.io') || window.location.pathname.startsWith('/esstu-schedule');
      const base = isGhPages ? '/esstu-schedule/api' : '/api';
      if (!isGhPages) {
        return path;
      }
      if (path === '/api/catalog-status') return `${base}/catalog-status.json`;
      if (path === '/api/rooms') return `${base}/rooms.json`;
      // GitHub Pages / Static JSON mapping
      if (path.startsWith('/api/groups')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/groups/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/schedule/')) {
        const parts = path.replace('/api/schedule/', '').split('?')[0].split('/');
        return `${base}/schedule/${parts[0]}/${parts[1]}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/departments')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/departments/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/teachers/all')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/teachers/all/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/department/')) {
        const parts = path.replace('/api/department/', '').split('?')[0].split('/');
        return `${base}/department/${parts[0]}/${parts[1]}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/status')) {
        return `${base}/status.json`;
      }
      return path;
    }

    async function loadGroups(section = 'bakalavriat') {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`));
        const data = await res.json();
        allGroups = data.groups || [];
        renderSectionTabs();
        renderGroupsModalList();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Ошибка загрузки групп:', e);
      }
    }

    async function loadStudentSchedule(section, groupId, groupName = null, forceFresh = false) {
      const version=beginScheduleLoad(),previous=currentSchedule;
      try {
        if(groupName){
          const catalogResponse=await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`),{cache:forceFresh?'reload':'default'});
          if(!catalogResponse.ok)throw Error('Каталог групп недоступен');
          const catalog=(await catalogResponse.json()).groups || [];
          const actual=catalog.find(group=>normSearch(group.name)===normSearch(groupName));
          if(!actual)throw Error('Выбранная группа не найдена в каталоге');
          groupId=actual.id;
        }
        const nameParam = groupName ? `?group_name=${encodeURIComponent(groupName)}` : '';
        const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/schedule/${section}/${encodeURIComponent(groupId)}${nameParam}`), {cache:forceFresh ? 'reload' : 'default'});
        const data = await scheduleJSON(res);
        if(groupName && normSearch(data.group_name)!==normSearch(groupName))throw Error('Название группы не совпало с источником');
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        finishScheduleLoad(version);
        appMode = 'student';
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_mode', 'student');
        currentSchedule = data;
        trackSchedule(data);

        const finalName = data.group_name || groupName || groupId;
        const resolvedId = data.resolved_group_id || data.group_id || groupId;
        savedState.section = section;
        savedState.groupId = resolvedId;
        savedState.groupName = finalName;

        localStorage.setItem('esstu_section', section);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_group_id', resolvedId);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_group_name', finalName);

        if (data.last_modified) {
          document.getElementById('lastModifiedText').textContent = `Источник: portal.esstu.ru • ${data.last_modified}`;
        }
        rememberRecentSchedule();
        renderAll();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Ошибка загрузки расписания:', e);
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(forceFresh && previous){currentSchedule={...previous,is_stale_fallback:true};renderAll();showToast('Источник недоступен. Показана сохранённая версия.');}
        else{currentSchedule=null;document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent=groupName || groupId;scheduleUnavailable('Источник расписания недоступен');}
        return false;
      } finally {finishScheduleLoad(version);}
    }

    let teacherDirectoryLoading = null;
    let teacherDirectoryError = '';
    let selectedCafFilter = '';

    function normSearch(value) {
      return String(value || '').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase().replace(/ё/g, 'е').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    }
    function teacherMatches(t, query, caf = '') {
      if (caf && !(t.caf_ids || [t.caf_id]).some(id => normSearch(id) === normSearch(caf))) return false;
      const p = t.profile || {};
      const lessons = Object.values(t.weeks || {}).flatMap(days => Object.values(days).flatMap(d => (d.pairs || []).flatMap(p => p.lessons || [])));
      const text = normSearch([t.teacher_key, p.full_name, p.short_name, p.department, p.degree, p.building, ...(t.caf_ids || [t.caf_id]),
        ...lessons.map(l => `${l.subject || ''} ${l.room || ''} ${decodeEsstuRoom(l.room)?.building || ''}`)].join(' '));
      return normSearch(query).split(' ').every(word => text.includes(word));
    }
    async function loadDepartmentsAndTeachers(forceFresh = false) {
      if (teacherDirectoryLoading) return teacherDirectoryLoading;
      teacherDirectoryLoading = (async () => {
        teacherDirectoryError = '';
        try {
          const results = await Promise.allSettled([
            fetch(getApiUrl('/api/departments?section=bakalavriat')).then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw Error(); return r.json(); }),
            fetch(getApiUrl('/api/teachers/all?section=bakalavriat'),{cache:forceFresh ? 'reload' : 'default'}).then(scheduleJSON),
          ]);
          if (results[0].status === 'fulfilled') departmentsHierarchy = results[0].value.institutes || [];
          if (results[1].status === 'fulfilled') allTeachersDirectory = (results[1].value.teachers || []).map(t=>({...t,_delivery:results[1].value._delivery}));
          else if (!allTeachersDirectory.length) teacherDirectoryError = 'Не удалось загрузить преподавателей. Проверьте соединение и повторите загрузку.';
          if (forceFresh && results[1].status !== 'fulfilled') throw Error('Не удалось проверить новую версию каталога');
        } finally { teacherDirectoryLoading = null; renderTeacherModalContent(); }
      })();
      renderTeacherModalContent();
      return teacherDirectoryLoading;
    }

    async function loadTeacherSchedule(teacherKey, cafId = '', forceFresh = false) {
      const version=beginScheduleLoad(),previous=currentSchedule;
      try {
        if (forceFresh || !allTeachersDirectory.length) await loadDepartmentsAndTeachers(forceFresh);
        let found = allTeachersDirectory.find(t => normSearch(t.teacher_key) === normSearch(teacherKey));
        if (!found && cafId) {
          const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/department/bakalavriat/${encodeURIComponent(cafId)}?merge=true`));
          if (res.ok) found = ((await res.json()).teachers || []).find(t => normSearch(t.teacher_key) === normSearch(teacherKey));
        }
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(!found)throw Error('Преподаватель не найден в сохранённых расписаниях');
        appMode = 'teacher'; localStorage.setItem('esstu_mode', 'teacher');
        savedState.teacherKey = found.teacher_key; savedState.teacherCafId = found.caf_id || '';
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_teacher_key', savedState.teacherKey);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_teacher_caf', savedState.teacherCafId);
        currentSchedule = found; trackSchedule(found);finishScheduleLoad(version);rememberRecentSchedule();renderAll();return true;
      }catch(error){
        console.error(error);if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(forceFresh && previous){currentSchedule={...previous,is_stale_fallback:true};renderAll();showToast('Не удалось обновить преподавателя.');}
        else{currentSchedule=null;scheduleUnavailable('Расписание преподавателя недоступно');}
        return false;
      }finally{finishScheduleLoad(version);}
    }

    function openSelectorModal() {
      if (appMode === 'student') toggleGroupModal(true);
      else toggleTeacherModal(true);
    }

    function openTeacherFromLesson(teacherKey, cafId) {
      if (!teacherKey) return;
      loadTeacherSchedule(teacherKey, cafId || 'Caf5');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }

    function toggleGroupModal(show) { if (show) openSearchModal('student'); else closeSearchModal(); }
    function toggleTeacherModal(show) { if (show) openSearchModal('teacher'); else closeSearchModal(); }

    function changeStudentSection(sec) {
      savedState.section = sec;
      loadGroups(sec);
    }

    function renderSectionTabs() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.sec-btn').forEach((btn) => {
        const s = btn.getAttribute('data-sec');
        btn.className = s === savedState.section
          ? 'sec-btn shrink-0 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white'
          : 'sec-btn shrink-0 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300';
      });
    }

    function setCourseFilter(course) {
      selectedCourseFilter = course;
      document.querySelectorAll('.course-btn').forEach((btn) => {
        const c = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-course'));
        btn.className = c === course
          ? 'course-btn shrink-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white'
          : 'course-btn shrink-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800';
      });
      renderGroupsModalList();
    }

    function renderGroupsModalList() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('groupSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = document.getElementById('groupsListContainer');

      const filtered = allGroups.filter((g) => {
        if (selectedCourseFilter !== 0 && g.course !== selectedCourseFilter) return false;
        if (q && !g.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
        return true;
      }).sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.course)||99)-(Number(b.course)||99) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'ru',{numeric:true}));

      if (!filtered.length) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-400 py-6">Группы не найдены</div>`;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = filtered.map((g) => `
        <button
          onclick="chooseGroup('${g.section}', '${g.id}', '${g.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')"
          aria-pressed="${g.section===savedState.section && g.name===savedState.groupName && appMode==='student'}"
          class="p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-left transition ${
            g.name === savedState.groupName && appMode === 'student'
              ? 'bg-teal-600/15 border-teal-500'
              : 'bg-slate-100/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/70 hover:border-teal-500/50'
          }"
        >
          <div class="group-name">${escapeHTML(g.name)}${g.section===savedState.section && g.name===savedState.groupName && appMode==='student'?`<span class="group-current">${uiIcon('check')}<span class="sr-only">Выбрана</span></span>`:''}</div>
          <div class="group-course">${g.course} курс</div>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    }

    function chooseGroup(section, id, name) {
      toggleGroupModal(false);
      loadStudentSchedule(section, id, name);
    }

    function renderTeacherModalContent() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = document.getElementById('teacherModalContent');

      const filteredTeachers = allTeachersDirectory.filter(t => teacherMatches(t, q, selectedCafFilter));

      visibleTeacherChoices = filteredTeachers;
      const teachersCards = filteredTeachers.map((t, teacherIndex) => {
        const p = t.profile || {};
        return `
          <button
            onclick="chooseTeacherByIndex(${teacherIndex})"
            class="w-full p-2.5 rounded-2xl border flex items-center gap-3 text-left transition ${
              t.teacher_key === savedState.teacherKey && appMode === 'teacher'
                ? 'bg-teal-600/15 border-teal-500'
                : 'bg-slate-100/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/70 hover:border-teal-500/50'
            }"
          >
            <img src="${escapeHTML(teacherAvatarURL(p))}" data-name="${escapeHTML(p.short_name || p.full_name)}" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src=initialsAvatar(this.dataset.name)" alt="" class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl object-cover shrink-0 bg-slate-700" />
            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div class="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">${escapeHTML(p.full_name || p.short_name)}</div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">${escapeHTML(p.degree || "")}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-teal-500">${getTeacherLiveStatus(t)}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">${escapeHTML((t.caf_ids?.length ? t.caf_ids : [t.caf_id]).filter(Boolean).map(id => `🏛 ${id}.htm`).join(' • ') || '📄 По расписаниям групп')}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 truncate">${escapeHTML(p.department)}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">${escapeHTML([...new Set(Object.values(t.weeks || {}).flatMap(w => Object.values(w).flatMap(d => (d.pairs || []).flatMap(p => (p.lessons || []).map(l => l.subject)))))].join(', '))}</div>
            </div>
          </button>
        `;
      }).join('');

      const deptsHtml = departmentsHierarchy.map((inst, instituteIndex) => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100/60 dark:bg-slate-800/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-1.5">
          <div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400">${escapeHTML(inst.institute)}</div>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            ${(inst.departments || []).map((d, departmentIndex) => `
              <button
                onclick="filterCafByIndex(${instituteIndex},${departmentIndex})"
                class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-medium bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-teal-500 transition"
              >
                🏛 ${escapeHTML(d.name)} <span class="opacity-60">(${escapeHTML(d.caf_id)})</span>
              </button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="space-y-2">
          <div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Преподаватели (${filteredTeachers.length})
            ${selectedCafFilter ? `<span class="normal-case text-teal-500">• ${escapeHTML(selectedCafFilter)}</span>` : ''}
            <button onclick="selectedCafFilter='';document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value='';renderTeacherModalContent()" class="ml-2 text-teal-500">Сбросить фильтры</button>
          </div>
          <div class="space-y-2">${teachersCards || `<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-3 text-center">${teacherDirectoryLoading ? 'Загрузка преподавателей…' : escapeHTML(teacherDirectoryError || 'По запросу преподаватели не найдены. Попробуйте другую фамилию или предмет.')}<button onclick="loadDepartmentsAndTeachers()" class="block mx-auto mt-2 text-teal-500">Повторить загрузку</button></div>`}</div>
        </div>
        <details class="pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-2">
          <summary class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 cursor-pointer py-2">Институты и кафедры ВСГУТУ</summary>
          ${deptsHtml}
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Часть расписаний преподавателей восстановлена из расписаний групп и может быть неполной.</p>
        </details>
      `;
    }

    function filterByCaf(cafId) {
      selectedCafFilter = cafId;
      document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value = '';
      renderTeacherModalContent();
    }

    }

    function selectWeek(w) { selectedWeek = w; renderAll(); }

    function saveFiltersAndRender() {
      const hideEmpty = true;
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_hide_empty', String(hideEmpty));
      renderAll();
    }

    function toggleTimeSim() {
      document.getElementById('timeSimPanel').classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function applyTimeSim() {
      const dayIndex = Number(document.getElementById('simDay').value);
      const timeVal = document.getElementById('simTime').value || '09:30';
      const [hours, minutes] = timeVal.split(':').map(Number);
      simOverride = { dayIndex, hours, minutes };
      selectedDayKey = DAY_KEYS[dayIndex];
      renderAll();
    }

    function resetTimeSim() {
      simOverride = null;
      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';
      renderAll();
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
      applyTheme();

      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';

      setInterval(updateLiveHero, 1000);
      const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
      if (params.has('group')) {
        appMode = 'student';
        const requested=params.get('group'),section=params.get('section') || 'bakalavriat';
        try {
          if(!['bakalavriat','spezialitet','zo1','zo2'].includes(section))throw Error('Неизвестный раздел в ссылке');
          const response=await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`));
          if(!response.ok)throw Error('Каталог групп недоступен');
          const groups=(await response.json()).groups || [];
          const group=groups.find(g=>normSearch(g.name)===normSearch(requested) || String(g.id)===requested);
          if(!group)throw Error('Группа из ссылки не найдена');
          savedState.section=section;savedState.groupId=group.id;savedState.groupName=group.name;
        }catch(error){
          startupLinkFailed=true;
          document.getElementById('pairsContainer').setAttribute('aria-busy','false');
          document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent=requested || 'Выберите группу';
          scheduleUnavailable(error.message);loadGroups(savedState.section);loadDepartmentsAndTeachers();return;
        }
      } else if (params.has('teacher')) {
        appMode = 'teacher'; savedState.teacherKey = params.get('teacher');
        savedState.teacherCafId = params.get('caf') || 'Caf5';
      }
      loadGroups(savedState.section);
      loadDepartmentsAndTeachers();

      if (appMode === 'teacher') {
        await loadTeacherSchedule(savedState.teacherKey, savedState.teacherCafId);
      } else {
        await loadStudentSchedule(savedState.section, savedState.groupId, savedState.groupName);
      }


      await handleUrlShortcuts();


    });
  </script>
