class ParserContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_frozen_demo_goldens(self):
        for case in json.loads(
            (FIXTURES / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        ):
            with self.subTest(source=case["source"]):
                raw = (FIXTURES / case["html"]).read_bytes()
                self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), case["sha256"])
                html = raw.decode("cp1251")
                with patch.object(parser, "load_teachers_meta", return_value={}):
                    if case["kind"] == "group":
                        actual = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(
                            html, case["id"], case["section"]
                        )
                    else:
                        actual = parser.parse_department_teachers_html(
                            html, case["id"], case["section"]
                        )[0]
                expected = json.loads(
                    (FIXTURES / case["json"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                )
                self.assertEqual(actual, expected)

    def test_correspondence_sunday_and_evening_time_are_in_source(self):
        raw = (FIXTURES / "zo1-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        result = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(raw, "1", "zo1")
        sunday = result["weeks"]["1"]["date_12.04"]
        self.assertEqual(sunday["day_index"], 6)
        self.assertTrue(any(pair["lessons"] for pair in sunday["pairs"]))
        self.assertEqual(result["bells"][-1]["number"], 7)
        self.assertEqual(result["bells"][-1]["start"], "19:40")

    def test_font_colour_cannot_change_week(self):
        html = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        original = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(html)
        changed = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(
            html.replace("#0000FF", "#ff0000").replace("#0000ff", "#ff0000")
        )
        self.assertFalse(original["is_incomplete"])
        self.assertEqual(original, changed)

    def test_truncated_and_unknown_structures_are_explicit(self):
        html = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        for kind in ("missing_day", "unknown_day", "bad_time"):
            soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
            rows = soup.table.find_all("tr")
            if kind == "missing_day":
                rows[3].decompose()
            elif kind == "unknown_day":
                rows[3].td.string = "???"
            else:
                rows[1].find_all("td")[1].string = "unknown"
            reset_diagnostics()
            with self.assertLogs("esstu_schedule", level="WARNING"):
                result = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(str(soup))
            self.assertTrue(result["is_incomplete"])
            self.assertTrue(result["parse_errors"])
            self.assertEqual(result["weeks"], {"1": {}, "2": {}})
            self.assertEqual(parsing_status()["parse_error_count"], 1)

    def test_explicit_headers_and_cell_weekdays(self):
        html = "<table><tr><td>Пары</td><td>1-я</td></tr><tr><td>Время</td><td>09:00–10:35</td></tr>"
        html += '<tr><td colspan="2">II неделя</td></tr><tr><td>Втр</td><td>лек. B</td></tr>'
        html += '<tr><td colspan="2">I неделя</td></tr><tr><td>Птн</td><td>лек. A</td></tr></table>'
        parsed = parser.parse_14row_table(BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml").table)
        self.assertIn("tuesday", parsed["weeks"]["2"])
        self.assertIn("friday", parsed["weeks"]["1"])
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("Вск,12 апреля", 0)[3], 6)
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("Втр,07 апреля", 5)[3], 1)
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("15 янв", 5)[3], -1)

    def test_metadata_external_edit_invalidates_cache(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as folder:
            file = Path(folder) / "meta.json"
            file.write_text("{}")
            parser._read_teachers_meta.cache_clear()
            with patch.object(parser, "TEACHERS_META_PATH", file):
                parser.load_teachers_meta()
                file.write_text(
                    '{"иванов и.и.":{"degree":"Professor"}}', encoding="utf-8"
                )
                os.utime(
                    file,
                    ns=(file.stat().st_atime_ns, file.stat().st_mtime_ns + 1000000),
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    parser.build_teacher_profile("Иванов И.И.")["degree"], "Professor"
                )

    def test_model_rejects_invalid_interval(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            validate_schedule(
                {
                    "weeks": {
                        "1": {
                            "monday": {
                                "pairs": [
                                    {
                                        "number": 1,
                                        "start": "10:35",
                                        "end": "09:00",
                                        "lessons": [],
                                    }
                                ]
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            )


class ArchitectureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_cold_cache_coalesces_and_force_refresh_waits(self):
        import time

        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        calls = []

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def example(key="A", force_refresh=False):
            calls.append(key)
            time.sleep(0.02)
            return {"version": len(calls)}

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
            results = list(pool.map(lambda _: example(), range(8)))
        self.assertEqual(len(calls), 1)
        self.assertTrue(all(item["version"] == 1 for item in results))
        self.assertEqual(example(force_refresh=True)["version"], 2)

    def test_failed_refresh_does_not_renew_stale_timestamp(self):
        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        now = [20000.0]
        calls = []

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def failure_case():
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) > 1:
                raise RuntimeError("portal unavailable")
            return {"value": 1}

        with patch.object(cache_tools.time, "time", side_effect=lambda: now[0]):
            failure_case()
            now[0] += 901
            with self.assertLogs(cache_tools.__name__, level="WARNING"):
                failure_case()
                import time

                deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
                while cache_tools._refreshing and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    threading.Event().wait(0.01)
            self.assertFalse(cache_tools._refreshing)
            self.assertEqual(
                next(iter(cache_tools._service_cache._data.values()))[0], 20000.0
            )
            now[0] += 3000
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                failure_case()

    def test_rooms_http_service_never_scans_schedules(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "rooms.json"
            write_json_atomic(
                path, {"count": 1, "rooms": [{"room": "0410", "slots": {}}]}
            )
            with (
                patch.dict(os.environ, {"ESSTU_ROOMS_INDEX": str(path)}),
                patch.object(
                    app, "service_all_teachers", side_effect=AssertionError("scan")
                ),
                patch.object(app, "service_groups", side_effect=AssertionError("scan")),
            ):
                self.assertEqual(app.service_rooms_index()["count"], 1)
            write_json_atomic(path, {"count": 0, "rooms": []})
            self.assertEqual(read_rooms_index(path)["count"], 0)
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                read_rooms_index(Path(folder) / "missing.json")

    def test_stale_refresh_is_nonblocking_and_coalesced(self):
        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        now = [10000.0]
        calls = []
        entered = threading.Event()
        release = threading.Event()
        done = threading.Event()

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def service(key="A"):
            calls.append(key)
            if len(calls) > 1:
                entered.set()
                release.wait(3)
            return {"value": len(calls)}

        with patch.object(cache_tools.time, "time", side_effect=lambda: now[0]):
            self.assertEqual(service()["value"], 1)
            now[0] += 901
            first = service()
            self.assertEqual(first["cache_state"], "stale_while_revalidate")
            self.assertTrue(entered.wait(1))
            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
                results = list(pool.map(lambda _: service(), range(16)))
            self.assertTrue(all(result["value"] == 1 for result in results))
            self.assertEqual(len(calls), 2)
            release.set()
            cache_tools._executor.submit(done.set)
            # Wait for the per-key flag to clear, not for a timing assumption.
            import time

            deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
            while cache_tools._refreshing and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                threading.Event().wait(0.01)
            self.assertFalse(cache_tools._refreshing)
            self.assertEqual(service()["value"], 2)

def test_real_headings_do_not_depend_on_font_colour():
    html = (FIXTURES / "zo1-24.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
    original = parse_group_schedule_html(html, "24", "zo1")
    assert original["group_name"] == "ЗУ-424-2"
    changed = parse_group_schedule_html(html.replace("#ff00ff", "#123456"), "24", "zo1")
    assert changed == original
    assert not changed["is_incomplete"]


def test_real_caf48_mixed_encoding_has_complete_teachers():
    raw = (FIXTURES / "spezialitet-Caf48.htm").read_bytes()
    teachers = parse_department_teachers_html(
        raw.decode("cp1251"), "Caf48", "spezialitet"
    )
    assert teachers and all(not teacher["is_incomplete"] for teacher in teachers)
    assert any("Скуратова" in teacher["profile"]["short_name"] for teacher in teachers)
    assert repair_mojibake("Р§С‚РІ") == "Чтв"
    assert repair_mojibake("РџРЅРґ") == "Пнд"
    assert repair_mojibake("История России Иванов И.И.") == "История России Иванов И.И."
    for fixture in json.loads(
        (FIXTURES / "review-manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ):
        assert (
            hashlib.sha256((FIXTURES / fixture["fixture"]).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            == fixture["sha256"]
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("age,used", [(0, True), (7, True), (8, False), (None, False)])
def test_group_fallback_has_identity_and_seven_day_expiry(age, used):
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    candidate = {
        "group_id": "new",
        "group_name": "A",
        "is_incomplete": True,
        "parse_errors": ["missing"],
    }
    old = {
        "group_id": "old",
        "group_name": "A",
        "is_incomplete": False,
        "weeks": {"1": {}},
        "source_checked_at": (now - timedelta(days=age)).isoformat()
        if age is not None
        else None,
    }
    result = last_good_group(candidate, old, now)
    assert bool(result.get("is_stale_fallback")) == used
    assert old["group_id"] == "old"
    assert not last_good_group({**candidate, "group_name": "B"}, old, now).get(
        "is_stale_fallback"
    )
    assert source_is_recent(old, now) == used


def test_quality_checks_regression_and_absolute_ceiling(tmp_path):
    baseline = tmp_path / "previous"
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    for root, failed in [(baseline, 20), (site, 21)]:
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
        (root / "api/status.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"group_expected_count": 100, "group_failed_count": failed})
        )
    check_parse_quality(site, baseline=baseline)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"group_expected_count": 100, "group_failed_count": 22})
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        check_parse_quality(site, baseline=baseline)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"group_expected_count": 100, "group_failed_count": 26})
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        check_parse_quality(site)


def test_code_publication_reuses_old_api_without_faking_freshness(tmp_path):
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    baseline = tmp_path / "old"
    for root in (site, baseline):
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    old = {
        "generated_at": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z",
        "group_failed_count": 121,
        "build_id": "old",
        "code_revision": "old",
    }
    (baseline / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(old))
    (baseline / "api/test.json").write_bytes(b'{"real old data":true}')
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "build_id": "tested",
                "code_revision": "new",
                "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
            }
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        reuse_api(site, baseline, "wrong")
    reuse_api(site, baseline, "tested")
    result = json.loads((site / "api/status.json").read_text())
    assert (
        result["generated_at"] == old["generated_at"]
        and result["group_failed_count"] == 121
    )
    assert result["build_id"] == "tested" and result["code_revision"] == "new"
    assert (site / "api/test.json").read_bytes() == (
        baseline / "api/test.json"
    ).read_bytes()


def saved_source(root, expected="1146 тпуп", recent=False):
    directory = root / "bakalavriat"
    directory.mkdir()
    (directory / "raspisan.htm").write_text(
        '<table><tr><td>1 курс</td></tr><tr><td><a href="1.htm">'
        + expected
        + "</a></td></tr></table>",
        encoding="cp1251",
    )
    raw = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes()
    (directory / "1.htm").write_bytes(raw)
    checked = datetime.now(UTC) if recent else datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    (root / "sources.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "bakalavriat/1.htm": {
                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                    "retrieved_at": checked.isoformat(),
                    "last_modified": "Fri, 16 Oct 2026 07:52:00 GMT",
                    "etag": "old-tag",
                }
            }
        )
    )
    return raw


def test_recent_valid_source_is_resumed_without_a_download(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path, recent=True)
    network = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", network)
    _, metadata = sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    network.assert_not_called()
    assert metadata["reused"]
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_not_modified_keeps_bytes_and_updates_checked_at(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path)
    seen = []

    def response(request, timeout):
        seen.append(request)
        raise HTTPError(request.full_url, 304, "Not Modified", {}, None)

    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", response)
    _, metadata = sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert seen[0].get_header("If-none-match") == "old-tag"
    assert len(seen) == 1 and metadata["bytes"] == len(raw) and metadata["reused"]
    assert (
        datetime.now(UTC) - datetime.fromisoformat(metadata["retrieved_at"])
    ).total_seconds() < 5
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_fetch_rejects_wrong_group_before_replacing_saved_source(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path, expected="Другая группа")

    class Response:
        headers = {}

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

        def read(self):
            return raw

    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", lambda *args, **kwargs: Response())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="identity"):
        sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_durable_data_branch_round_trip(tmp_path):
    from scripts.data_branch import restore, save, git

    remote = tmp_path / "remote.git"
    remote.mkdir()
    git(remote, "init", "--bare", "-q")
    sources = tmp_path / "source"
    sources.mkdir()
    (sources / "bytes.htm").write_bytes(b"raw\r\n\xff")
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    (site / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text('{"build_id":"checked"}')
    work = tmp_path / "writer"
    work.mkdir()
    git(work, "init", "-q")
    git(work, "config", "core.autocrlf", "false")
    git(work, "remote", "add", "origin", str(remote))
    save(work, site, sources, "revision")
    target = tmp_path / "reader"
    restored = tmp_path / "restored"
    assert restore(target, str(remote), restored)
    assert (restored / "bytes.htm").read_bytes() == b"raw\r\n\xff"
    assert json.loads((target / "api/status.json").read_text())["build_id"] == "checked"


def test_partial_group_errors_do_not_block_all_healthy_data(tmp_path):
    from scripts.check_sync import check_reports

    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(
            json.dumps({"downloaded": ["valid.htm"], "failed": [{"path": "bad.htm"}]})
        )
    check_reports(tmp_path, allow_partial=True)
    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(
            json.dumps({"downloaded": [], "failed": [{"path": "bad.htm"}]})
        )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="No source"):
        check_reports(tmp_path, allow_partial=True)


def test_bootstrap_reads_one_published_release_and_detects_a_mid_copy_update(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    from scripts.published_data import bootstrap
    from urllib.parse import urlparse

    values = {
        "api/status.json": {
            "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
            "code_revision": "previous",
        }
    }
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        values["api/groups/" + section + ".json"] = {
            "groups": [{"id": "1", "name": "A"}]
        }
        values["api/schedule/" + section + "/1.json"] = {"is_incomplete": False}
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet"):
        values["api/departments/" + section + ".json"] = {"institutes": []}
        values["api/teachers/all/" + section + ".json"] = {"teachers": []}
    for name in ("rooms", "sources", "catalog-status"):
        values["api/" + name + ".json"] = {}

    class Response:
        def __init__(self, value):
            self.value = value

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

        def read(self):
            return json.dumps(self.value).encode()

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "urllib.request.urlopen",
        lambda request, timeout: Response(
            values[urlparse(request.full_url).path.removeprefix("/site/")]
        ),
    )
    bootstrap(tmp_path / "valid", "https://test.invalid/site/")
    assert (
        json.loads((tmp_path / "valid/api/status.json").read_text())[
            "group_expected_count"
        ]
        == 4
    )
    calls = 0

    def changing(request, timeout):
        nonlocal calls
        path = urlparse(request.full_url).path.removeprefix("/site/")
        if path == "api/status.json":
            calls += 1
            return Response({**values[path], "code_revision": str(calls)})
        return Response(values[path])

    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", changing)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="changed during bootstrap"):
        bootstrap(tmp_path / "invalid", "https://test.invalid/site/")
    assert not (tmp_path / "invalid/api/status.json").exists()


===== tests/test_publication_quality.py =====
SHA-256: 5c59d3fbb3fd251d537e02d2ed89c7954fdf4f2b52954b703a266624b78b4c91

import json
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality
from scripts import sync_departments, sync_esstu


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "failed,blocked", [(0, False), (5, False), (6, True), (100, True)]
)
def test_publication_error_rate_limit(tmp_path, failed, blocked):
    (tmp_path / "api").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"group_expected_count": 100, "group_failed_count": failed})
    )
    if blocked:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Publication blocked"):
            check_parse_quality(tmp_path, max_error_rate=0.05)
    else:
        check_parse_quality(tmp_path, max_error_rate=0.05)

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "module,entry,expected",
    [(sync_departments, "sync", 2), (sync_esstu, "sync_groups", 4)],
)
def test_unavailable_live_catalog_never_fans_out_over_saved_pages(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, module, entry, expected
):
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        directory = tmp_path / section
        directory.mkdir()
        (
            directory / ("craspisanEdt.htm" if entry == "sync" else "raspisan.htm")
        ).write_text("saved catalog must not be used to fan out")
    fetch = Mock(side_effect=OSError("portal timed out"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "fetch_page", fetch)
    report = getattr(module, entry)()
    assert fetch.call_count == expected
    assert len(report["failed"]) == expected
    assert not report["downloaded"]

routedTest('mobile swipes move days and dismiss a sheet without losing focus',async({page,context,isMobile})=>{
  test.skip(!isMobile,'Native touch gestures are checked in the mobile project');await ready(page);
  await page.evaluate(()=>{selectedDayKey='tuesday';renderAll();});
  const cdp=await context.newCDPSession(page);
  async function swipe(x,y,endX,endY){
    await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent',{type:'touchStart',touchPoints:[{x,y}]});
    for(let step=1;step<=5;step++)await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent',{type:'touchMove',touchPoints:[{x:x+(endX-x)*step/5,y:y+(endY-y)*step/5}]});
    await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent',{type:'touchEnd',touchPoints:[]});
  }
  await page.locator('#dayTabs').evaluate(node=>node.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'instant'}));const tabs=await page.locator('#dayTabs').boundingBox();
  await swipe(tabs.x+tabs.width*.8,tabs.y+tabs.height/2,tabs.x+tabs.width*.2,tabs.y+tabs.height/2);
  await expect(page.locator('#dayTabs [aria-pressed="true"]')).toHaveAttribute('onclick',"selectDay('wednesday')");
  const launcher=page.getByRole('button',{name:'Ещё',exact:true});await launcher.click();
  const handle=page.locator('#moreMenuModal .sheet-handle');await expect(handle).toBeVisible();
  const grip=await handle.boundingBox();await swipe(grip.x+grip.width/2,grip.y+grip.height/2,grip.x+grip.width/2,grip.y+grip.height/2+130);
  await expect(page.locator('#moreMenuModal')).toBeHidden();await expect(launcher).toBeFocused();
});

test('a real worker update preserves saved data and the new shell opens offline',async({page,context})=>{
  const root=path.resolve('dist');let release=1;
  const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
    const url=new URL(req.url,'http://test');let name=decodeURIComponent(url.pathname).replace(/^\/esstu-schedule\//,'')||'index.html';
    const file=path.resolve(root,name);
    if(!file.startsWith(root+path.sep)||!fs.existsSync(file)||!fs.statSync(file).isFile()){res.writeHead(404);res.end();return;}
    let content=fs.readFileSync(file);
    if(name==='sw.js')content=Buffer.from(content.toString().replace(/const CACHE_NAME = '[^']+';/,`const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-browser-test-${release}';`));
    if(name==='index.html')content=Buffer.from(content.toString().replace(/content="[^"]+" name="app-version"/,`content="browser-test-${release}" name="app-version"`));
    if(name==='app.css')content=Buffer.from(content.toString()+`\n:root{--test-release:${release}}`);
    const mime={'.html':'text/html','.js':'application/javascript','.json':'application/json','.css':'text/css','.svg':'image/svg+xml','.png':'image/png'}[path.extname(file)]||'application/octet-stream';
    res.writeHead(200,{'Content-Type':mime,'Cache-Control':name==='sw.js'?'no-cache':'public, max-age=600'});res.end(content);
  });
  await new Promise(resolve=>server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve));
  const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/esstu-schedule/`;
  try{
    await ready(page,origin);await page.evaluate(()=>navigator.serviceWorker.ready);await ready(page,origin);
    await expect.poll(()=>page.evaluate(()=>!!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true);
    const peer=await context.newPage();await ready(peer,origin);
    await expect.poll(()=>peer.evaluate(()=>!!navigator.serviceWorker.controller)).toBe(true);
    await page.evaluate(()=>fetch('api/groups/bakalavriat.json').then(response=>response.json()));
    release=2;await page.evaluate(async()=>{const registration=await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration();await registration.update();});
    await expect(page.locator('#appUpdateBanner')).toBeVisible();
    const banner=await page.locator('#appUpdateBanner').boundingBox(),nav=await page.locator('.campus-nav').boundingBox();expect(banner.y+banner.height).toBeLessThan(nav.y);
    await Promise.all([page.waitForEvent('load'),peer.waitForEvent('load'),page.locator('#appUpdateBanner button').click()]);
    await expect(page.locator('#pairsContainer')).toHaveAttribute('aria-busy','false');
    await expect(page.locator('meta[name="app-version"]')).toHaveAttribute('content','browser-test-2');
    await expect(peer.locator('meta[name="app-version"]')).toHaveAttribute('content','browser-test-2');
    expect(await peer.evaluate(()=>getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--test-release').trim())).toBe('2');
    expect(await page.evaluate(()=>getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--test-release').trim())).toBe('2');
    const keys=await page.evaluate(()=>caches.keys());expect(keys.some(key=>key.endsWith('browser-test-2'))).toBe(true);expect(keys.some(key=>key.endsWith('browser-test-1'))).toBe(false);expect(keys.some(key=>key.endsWith('data-v2'))).toBe(true);
    await context.setOffline(true);await page.reload();
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading',{level:1})).toHaveText('1146 тпуп');
    await expect(page.locator('#compactFreshnessText')).toContainText('Офлайн');
    await expect(page.locator('#pairsContainer')).toHaveAttribute('aria-busy','false');
  }finally{server.closeAllConnections();await new Promise(resolve=>server.close(resolve));}
});

test('a slow API response refreshes the visible schedule in the background and remains offline',async({page,context})=>{
  const root=path.resolve('dist');let slow=false;
  const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
    const name=decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url,'http://test').pathname).replace(/^\/esstu-schedule\//,'')||'index.html',file=path.resolve(root,name);
    if(!file.startsWith(root+path.sep)||!fs.existsSync(file)||!fs.statSync(file).isFile()){res.writeHead(404);res.end();return;}
    let content=fs.readFileSync(file);const changed=slow && name==='api/schedule/bakalavriat/1.json';
    if(changed){const data=JSON.parse(content);data.weeks['1'].friday.pairs.find(pair=>pair.lessons.length).lessons[0].subject='Фоновое обновление расписания';content=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(data));}
    const send=()=>{const mime={'.html':'text/html','.js':'application/javascript','.json':'application/json','.css':'text/css','.svg':'image/svg+xml','.png':'image/png'}[path.extname(file)]||'application/octet-stream';res.writeHead(200,{'Content-Type':mime,'Cache-Control':'no-cache'});res.end(content);};
    if(changed)setTimeout(send,4800);else send();
  });
  await new Promise(resolve=>server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve));const origin=`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/esstu-schedule/`;
  try{
    await ready(page,origin);await page.evaluate(()=>navigator.serviceWorker.ready);await ready(page,origin);slow=true;
    const reason=await page.evaluate(async()=>{const response=await fetch('api/schedule/bakalavriat/1.json');await response.json();return response.headers.get('X-ESSTU-Cache');});
    expect(reason).toBe('refreshing');await expect(page.locator('#pairsContainer')).toContainText('Фоновое обновление расписания');
    await context.setOffline(true);await page.reload();await expect(page.locator('#pairsContainer')).toContainText('Фоновое обновление расписания');
  }finally{server.closeAllConnections();await new Promise(resolve=>server.close(resolve));}
});

===== e2e/contrast.spec.cjs =====
SHA-256: f69ad82fd0d2aaf0c3b500da8b701e7704dd69ea34a6a30b320019ddafd5532b

const {test,expect}=require('./fixtures.cjs');
const {PNG}=require('playwright-core/lib/utilsBundle');
const luminance=rgb=>rgb.map(v=>{v/=255;return v<=.04045?v/12.92:((v+.055)/1.055)**2.4;}).reduce((sum,v,i)=>sum+v*[.2126,.7152,.0722][i],0);
const contrast=(a,b)=>{const x=luminance(a),y=luminance(b);return (Math.max(x,y)+.05)/(Math.min(x,y)+.05);};
test('small labels meet 4.5 contrast against rendered gradients in both themes',async({page},testInfo)=>{
 await page.emulateMedia({reducedMotion:'reduce'});await page.goto('./');await expect(page.locator('#pairsContainer')).toHaveAttribute('aria-busy','false');const results=[];
 for(const theme of ['dark','light']){
  await page.evaluate(theme=>{themeMode=theme;applyTheme();},theme);
  for(const selector of ['.calendar-count','.pair-time>div:nth-child(3)','.lesson-warning .lesson-kind','#compactFreshnessText','.hero-lesson-meta']){
   const node=page.locator(selector).first();const style=await node.evaluate(node=>{const css=getComputedStyle(node);return {color:css.color,fontSize:css.fontSize,inline:node.getAttribute('style')};});
   const rgb=style.color.match(/[\d.]+/g).slice(0,3).map(Number);await node.evaluate(node=>node.style.setProperty('color','transparent','important'));
   let minimum=Infinity;
   try{const bitmap=PNG.sync.read(await node.screenshot({animations:'disabled'}));for(let i=0;i<bitmap.data.length;i+=4)if(bitmap.data[i+3]===255)minimum=Math.min(minimum,contrast(rgb,[...bitmap.data.subarray(i,i+3)]));}
   finally{await node.evaluate((node,inline)=>{if(inline===null)node.removeAttribute('style');else node.setAttribute('style',inline);},style.inline);}
   results.push({theme,selector,minContrast:minimum,fontSize:style.fontSize});expect(minimum,theme+' '+selector).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);expect(parseFloat(style.fontSize)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(12);
  }
 }
 await testInfo.attach('rendered-contrast',{body:JSON.stringify(results,null,2),contentType:'application/json'});
});

test('radar accessibility, readable labels and responsive views in both themes',async({page},testInfo)=>{
  await ready(page);
  for(const theme of ['dark','light']){
    await page.evaluate(theme=>{themeMode=theme;applyTheme();},theme);
    await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Аудитории',exact:true}).click();
    await page.locator('#roomPair').selectOption('1');
    const result=await new AxeBuilder({page}).withTags(['wcag2a','wcag2aa','wcag21aa']).analyze();
    expect(result.violations).toEqual([]);
    for(const width of [320,390,768,1440]){
      await page.setViewportSize({width,height:900});
      expect(await page.locator('#roomsModal .simple-sheet').evaluate(node=>node.scrollWidth<=node.clientWidth)).toBe(true);
    }
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  }
  await page.emulateMedia({reducedMotion:'reduce'});
  await page.evaluate(()=>{themeMode='dark';applyTheme();});
  await page.setViewportSize({width:testInfo.project.name==='mobile'?390:1440,height:1000});
  fs.mkdirSync('preview/design-review',{recursive:true});
  await page.screenshot({path:`preview/design-review/day-${testInfo.project.name}.png`,fullPage:true,animations:'disabled'});
  await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Поиск',exact:true}).click();
  await page.screenshot({path:`preview/design-review/search-${testInfo.project.name}.png`,animations:'disabled'});
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Аудитории',exact:true}).click();
  await page.screenshot({path:`preview/design-review/rooms-${testInfo.project.name}.png`,animations:'disabled'});
});

  let stale=true;
  await page.route('**/api/status.json',route=>route.fulfill({json:{...status,generated_at:stale?'2026-10-15T00:00:00Z':'2026-10-16T01:00:00Z'}}));
  await ready(page);
  await expect(page.locator('#publicationFreshnessWarning')).toContainText('более 12 часов');
  stale=false;await ready(page);
  await expect(page.locator('#publicationFreshnessWarning')).toBeHidden();
});

const fs=require('fs'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('assert');
async function setup(controlled=true){
 const events={},docEvents={},timers=[],intervals=[],button={disabled:false,textContent:''};let reloads=0,checks=0;
 const registration={waiting:null,addEventListener(){},update(){checks++;return Promise.resolve();}};
 const context={console:{...console,warn(){}},updateLiveHero(){},window:{},document:{visibilityState:'visible',addEventListener(n,fn){docEvents[n]=fn;},getElementById(){return {classList:{remove(){}},...button};}},navigator:{serviceWorker:{controller:controlled?{}:null,addEventListener(n,fn){events[n]=fn;},register:async(url,options)=>{assert.equal(options.updateViaCache,'none');return registration;}}},location:{reload(){reloads++;}},setTimeout(fn,ms){timers.push({fn,ms});return timers.length;},clearTimeout(){},setInterval(fn,ms){intervals.push({fn,ms});}};
 vm.createContext(context);vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync('static/experience.js','utf8'),context);await vm.runInContext('initAppUpdates()',context);
 return {context,events,docEvents,timers,intervals,button,get reloads(){return reloads;},get checks(){return checks;},run(code){return vm.runInContext(code,context);}};
}
(async()=>{
 let app=await setup();app.events.controllerchange();app.events.controllerchange();assert.equal(app.reloads,1,'controlled peer reloads once without clicking');
 app=await setup(false);app.events.controllerchange();assert.equal(app.reloads,0,'first installation does not reload');app.events.controllerchange();assert.equal(app.reloads,1);
 app=await setup();assert.equal(app.intervals[0].ms,900000);app.context.document.visibilityState='hidden';app.intervals[0].fn();assert.equal(app.checks,0);app.context.document.visibilityState='visible';app.docEvents.visibilitychange();assert.equal(app.checks,1);

const fs=require('fs'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('assert');
const scope='https://example.test/esstu-schedule/', prefix=`esstu-pwa:${scope}:`;
const handlers={},stores=new Map(),deleted=[],timeouts=[],installRequests=[];
let offline=false,status=200,failPut=false,fetches=0,skipped=false,deferred=null;
const notifications=[],backgroundJobs=[];
const key=request=>new URL(typeof request==='string'?request:request.url,scope).href;
function store(name){
  if(!stores.has(name)){
    const entries=new Map();
    stores.set(name,{
      async match(request,options={}){
        const url=key(request);
        const found=[...entries].find(([candidate])=>options.ignoreSearch?candidate.split('?')[0]===url.split('?')[0]:candidate===url);
        return found?.[1].clone();
      },
      async put(request,response){if(failPut)throw Error('quota');entries.set(key(request),response.clone());},
      async delete(request){return entries.delete(key(request));},
      async keys(){return [...entries.keys()].map(url=>new Request(url));},
      async addAll(assets){for(const request of assets){assert(request instanceof Request);assert.equal(request.cache,'reload');assert(request.url.startsWith(scope));installRequests.push(request);await this.put(request,new Response('installed shell'));}}
    });
  }
  return stores.get(name);
}
const context={self:{addEventListener:(name,fn)=>handlers[name]=fn,location:{origin:new URL(scope).origin},registration:{scope},clients:{claim:async()=>{},matchAll:async()=>[{postMessage:message=>notifications.push(message)}]},skipWaiting:async()=>{skipped=true}},
  caches:{open:async name=>store(name),keys:async()=>[...stores.keys()],delete:async name=>{deleted.push(name);return stores.delete(name);}},
  fetch:async(request,options)=>{fetches++;assert.equal(options.cache,'reload');if(offline)throw Error('offline');if(deferred)return deferred;return new Response('network',{status});},
  Request,Response,Headers,URL,setTimeout:(fn,ms)=>setTimeout(fn,ms===3500?10:ms),clearTimeout,AbortSignal:{timeout:ms=>{timeouts.push(ms);return AbortSignal.timeout(ms);}},console:{warn(){}}};
vm.createContext(context);vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync('static/sw.js','utf8'),context);
async function lifecycle(name,event={}){let job;handlers[name]({...event,waitUntil:p=>job=p});await job;}
async function request(path,options={}){let result;handlers.fetch({request:new Request(new URL(path,scope),options),waitUntil:p=>backgroundJobs.push(p),respondWith:p=>result=p});return result;}
(async()=>{

  await lifecycle('activate');

  status=200;assert.equal(await (await request('api/schedule?group=A')).text(),'network');

  assert.equal(await (await request('api/schedule?group=A')).text(),'network','reactivation preserves data');

// Exercise the actual notification script with an in-memory GitHub API.
const workflow=fs.readFileSync('.github/workflows/sync.yml','utf8').replace(/\r\n/g,'\n');
const script=workflow.split('          script: |\n')[1].split('\n').map(line=>line.slice(12)).join('\n');
async function notification(result,exists){
  const calls=[];const api={};
  for(const action of ['update','create'])api[action]=async data=>calls.push({action,...data});
  api.listForRepo=()=>{};
  const github={paginate:async()=>exists?[{number:8,title:'Автоматическая синхронизация расписания: ошибка'}]:[],rest:{issues:api}};
  await new Function('github','context','process',`return (async()=>{${script}})()`)(github,{repo:{owner:'test',repo:'test'},runId:123},{env:{SYNC_RESULT:result}});
  return calls;
}
(async()=>{
  const ci=fs.readFileSync('.github/workflows/ci.yml','utf8').replace(/\r\n/g,'\n');
  assert(ci.indexOf('Browser and accessibility regressions on frozen data')<ci.indexOf('Restore durable validated data'));
  assert(ci.indexOf('Browser and accessibility regressions on frozen data')<ci.indexOf('- name: Synchronize sources'));
  assert.equal((await notification('cancelled',false))[0].action,'create');
  assert.equal((await notification('failure',false))[0].action,'create');
  assert.equal((await notification('failure',true))[0].action,'update');
  assert.equal((await notification('success',true))[0].state,'closed');
  assert.deepEqual(await notification('success',false),[]);
  console.log('Source age, offline label, failure notification deduplication and recovery OK');
})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1});

  assert.deepEqual(document.errors,[],name);
  const workflow=document.toJS();workflows[name]=workflow;
  for(const [jobName,job] of Object.entries(workflow.jobs)){

    assert.equal(job['runs-on'],'ubuntu-24.04',`${name}/${jobName}`);

const assert=require('assert');
const {freshnessProblem,monitor}=require('../../scripts/freshness.cjs');
const now=Date.parse('2026-10-17T12:00:00+08:00');
const fresh={generated_at:new Date(now).toISOString(),source_checked_at_latest:new Date(now-3600000).toISOString()};
assert.equal(freshnessProblem(fresh,now),'');
assert(freshnessProblem({...fresh,generated_at:'2026-10-16T12:00:00+08:00'},now).includes('Сборка: более 12 часов'));
assert(freshnessProblem({...fresh,source_checked_at_latest:'2026-10-16T12:00:00+08:00'},now).includes('Проверка источников: более 12 часов'));
assert(freshnessProblem({},now).includes('дата неизвестна'));
assert(freshnessProblem({...fresh,generated_at:'2026-10-18T12:00:00+08:00'},now).includes('дата в будущем'));
async function check(payload,existing,networkError=false){
  const calls=[];
  const api={listForRepo(){},create:async data=>calls.push({action:'create',...data}),update:async data=>calls.push({action:'update',...data})};
  const github={rest:{issues:api},paginate:async()=>existing?[{number:7,title:'Расписание: выгрузка устарела'}]:[]};
  await monitor({github,context:{repo:{owner:'test',repo:'schedule'},runId:123},now,
    fetchImpl:async(url,options)=>{assert(url.includes('?freshness='));assert.equal(options.cache,'no-store');if(networkError)throw Error('unavailable');return {ok:true,json:async()=>payload};}});
  return calls;
}
(async()=>{
  assert.deepEqual(await check(fresh,false),[]);
  assert.equal((await check({},false))[0].action,'create');
  assert.equal((await check({},true))[0].action,'update');
  assert.equal((await check(fresh,true))[0].state,'closed');
  assert((await check(fresh,false,true))[0].body.includes('unavailable'));
  console.log('Freshness: 12-hour threshold, source age independent of build age, unknown/future timestamps, issue deduplication and recovery OK');
})().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});
